You delivered `app/strategy/trend_box.py`, `app/strategy/range_break.py` and their tests against

Start from `git diff` and `git status` to see them exactly.

## The four questions to answer

1. **Is turning the take-profit floor off actually safe?** The claim is that it rejected 1 of 496 signals
   (`gate_bite.py`). Check every place a take-profit floor still matters: `app/ai/plan.py`,
   `app/ai/trader.py` (`MIN_TAKE_PROFIT`), `app/strategy/ai_lane.py` (`target_trim` uses
   `min_take_profit` in `max(reach * (1 - trim), min(min_take_profit, reach))`). **With the floor at 0.0,
   does any of those expressions now behave differently from what its comment says, divide by zero, or
   let a target collapse onto the entry?** Write the arithmetic out, do not guess.

2. **Are the three new tests honest?** The research session caught one of its own tests re-implementing
   the production filter inside the test body, so a planted bug could not fail it. Check all three new
   tests for the same flaw: does each one fail if the production line it claims to cover is broken?
   Prove it by breaking the line and running the single test.

3. **Plant your own bugs against the NEW tests.** Pick at least 6 mistakes the research session's tests
   should catch and that are different from the 21 in your `plant_bugs.py` and the 14 in `my_bugs.py`.
   Aim at the newly covered ground: the `box * leg` cap, the H4 close-to-open shift, the forming-H4
   filter, the floor parameter. Write them as a script like `plant_bugs.py` with a crash-safe backup,
   run it, and report caught / MISSED.

4. **Anything else wrong.** `warmup_bars()` returning 1200 while `required_timeframes()` returns H1 -
   does the lane actually get 1200 H1 bars? Does the `three_bots` lane in `config.yaml` line up with
   what the strategies read? Is `RangeBreak.should_close` / `TrendBox.should_close` reachable at all
   given the decision timestamps the engine uses?

"""Confirmed-swing trend pullbacks with a closed M5 range break."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import time
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo

import pandas as pd
from pydantic import Field

from app.core.enums import Action, ExitReason, Side, Timeframe
from app.strategy.base_strategy import BaseStrategy, StrategyParams
from app.strategy.signal_models import (
    Condition,
    OpenPosition,
    PositionAdvice,
    ReasonCode,
    Signal,
    StrategyContext,
)
from app.strategy.zigzag import Swing, zigzag

BANGKOK = ZoneInfo("Asia/Bangkok")
NEW_YORK = ZoneInfo("America/New_York")


class TrendBoxParams(StrategyParams):
    """Measured constants shared by the two trend directions."""

    buffer: float = Field(default=0.20, ge=0, le=2)
    min_r: float = Field(default=1.2, gt=0, le=10)
    # Owner 2026-10-27: "you can take the 5 USD out". Measured first (research/.../gate_bite.py): the
    # 5.00 USD floor rejected 1 of 496 complete signals, because every target here is a swing or half an
    # H4 box. The gate stays so a lane can set one; 0.0 is off.
    min_take_profit: float = Field(default=0.0, ge=0)


class TrendBoxDownParams(TrendBoxParams):
    box: float = Field(default=0.20, gt=0, le=1)


class TrendBoxUpParams(TrendBoxParams):
    box: float = Field(default=0.33, gt=0, le=1)


def in_window(at: pd.Timestamp) -> bool:
    """Research windows use a start-exclusive, end-inclusive close timestamp."""
    utc = at.tz_convert("UTC")
    bangkok = utc.tz_convert(BANGKOK)
    new_york = utc.tz_convert(NEW_YORK)
    bt, nt = bangkok.time(), new_york.time()
    return (time(7) < bt <= time(9)) or (time(13) < bt <= time(15)) or (time(8) < nt <= time(11, 30))


def stepped(swings: list[Swing]) -> str:
    highs = [s.price for s in swings if s.kind == 1]
    lows = [s.price for s in swings if s.kind == -1]
    if len(highs) < 2 or len(lows) < 2:
        return "FLAT"
    if highs[-1] < highs[-2] and lows[-1] < lows[-2]:
        return "DOWN"
    if highs[-1] > highs[-2] and lows[-1] > lows[-2]:
        return "UP"
    return "FLAT"


def running_push_exceeded(run: float, risk: float) -> bool:
    """Apply the measured strict 1.2 R cutoff to the preceding M1 run."""
    return run > 1.2 * risk


def break_even_advice(ctx: StrategyContext, pos: OpenPosition) -> PositionAdvice | None:
    """Replay closed M1 bars so a stop crossed before a threshold touch keeps precedence."""
    if (pos.side is Side.BUY and pos.sl >= pos.entry) or (pos.side is Side.SELL and pos.sl <= pos.entry):
        return None
    m1 = ctx.frame(Timeframe.M1)
    bars = m1[(m1.index >= pd.Timestamp(pos.entry_time)) & (m1.index <= pd.Timestamp(ctx.decision_time_utc))]
    risk = abs(pos.initial_sl - pos.entry)
    if risk <= 0 or bars.empty:
        return None
    sell = pos.side is Side.SELL
    stop = pos.sl
    moved = False
    highs = bars["high"].to_numpy(dtype=float)
    lows = bars["low"].to_numpy(dtype=float)
    for high, low in zip(highs, lows, strict=True):
        if (high >= stop) if sell else (low <= stop):
            return None
        if pos.tp is not None and ((low <= pos.tp) if sell else (high >= pos.tp)):
            return None
        ahead = pos.entry - low if sell else high - pos.entry
        if ahead >= 0.5 * risk:
            stop = pos.entry
            moved = True
    if not moved:
        return None
    return PositionAdvice(new_sl=pos.entry, stop_kind=ExitReason.BREAK_EVEN, note="+0.5 R touched")


class TrendBox(BaseStrategy):
    """Shared confirmed H1 swing, M5 box and running-push gates."""

    Params = TrendBoxParams
    direction: str
    use_break_even: bool

    def required_timeframes(self) -> tuple[Timeframe, ...]:
        return Timeframe.M1, Timeframe.M5, Timeframe.H1

    def warmup_bars(self) -> int:
        return 400

    def evaluate(self, ctx: StrategyContext) -> Signal:
        at = pd.Timestamp(ctx.decision_time_utc)
        h1 = ctx.frame(Timeframe.H1).iloc[-400:]
        m5 = ctx.frame(Timeframe.M5)
        m1 = ctx.frame(Timeframe.M1)
        if len(h1) < 400 or len(m5) < 25 or not len(m1):
            return self.hold(ctx, ReasonCode.INSUFFICIENT_DATA)
        if not in_window(at):
            return self.hold(ctx, ReasonCode.OUTSIDE_SESSION)
        swings, _forming = zigzag(h1.high.to_numpy(float), h1.low.to_numpy(float))
        highs = [s for s in swings if s.kind == 1]
        lows = [s for s in swings if s.kind == -1]
        down = self.direction == "DOWN"
        stepping = (
            len(highs) >= 2
            and len(lows) >= 2
            and (
                (highs[-1].price < highs[-2].price and lows[-1].price < lows[-2].price)
                if down
                else (highs[-1].price > highs[-2].price and lows[-1].price > lows[-2].price)
            )
        )
        if not stepping or len(swings) < 2:
            return self.hold(ctx, "TREND_STEPPING")
        a, b = swings[-2:]
        if (a.kind, b.kind) != ((1, -1) if down else (-1, 1)):
            return self.hold(ctx, "LEG_ORDER")
        leg = abs(a.price - b.price)
        if leg <= 0:
            return self.hold(ctx, "ZERO_LEG")
        decision = m5.iloc[-1]
        price = float(decision.close)
        selected: tuple[int, float, float] | None = None
        for n in range(24, 5, -1):
            if len(m5) < n + 1:
                continue
            box = m5.iloc[-n - 1 : -1]
            top, floor = float(box.high.max()), float(box.low.min())
            if top - floor <= self.params.box * leg:
                selected = n, top, floor
                break
        if selected is None:
            return self.hold(ctx, "NO_BOX")
        _n, top, floor = selected
        mid = (top + floor) / 2
        retr = (mid - b.price) / leg if down else (b.price - mid) / leg
        if not 0.382 <= retr <= 0.786:
            return self.hold(ctx, "RETRACE")
        if (down and price >= floor) or (not down and price <= top):
            return self.hold(ctx, "NO_CLOSE_BREAK")
        stop = (
            top + self.params.buffer * (top - floor) if down else floor - self.params.buffer * (top - floor)
        )
        target = b.price
        risk, reward = abs(stop - price), abs(target - price)
        if reward < self.params.min_take_profit or reward < self.params.min_r * risk:
            return self.hold(ctx, ReasonCode.RR_TOO_LOW)
        if (down and target >= price) or (not down and target <= price) or risk <= 0:
            return self.hold(ctx, "INVALID_GEOMETRY")
        run_frame = m1[(m1.index >= at - pd.Timedelta(minutes=60)) & (m1.index < at)]
        if run_frame.empty:
            return self.hold(ctx, ReasonCode.INSUFFICIENT_DATA)
        run = price - float(run_frame.low.min()) if down else float(run_frame.high.max()) - price
        if running_push_exceeded(run, risk):
            return self.hold(ctx, "RUNNING_PUSH")
        action = Action.SELL if down else Action.BUY
        return self.entry(
            ctx,
            action,
            entry_price=price,
            stop_loss=stop,
            take_profit=target,
            conditions=(
                Condition("stepping", True),
                Condition("box", True, detail=f"{_n} bars"),
                Condition("retrace", True, detail=f"{retr:.3f}"),
                Condition("trigger", True),
                Condition("run_gate", True, detail=f"{run:.2f} <= {1.2 * risk:.2f}"),
            ),
            score=80,
            explanation=f"{self.direction.lower()} confirmed-swing box break",
        )

    def should_close(self, ctx: StrategyContext, side: Side) -> bool:
        at = pd.Timestamp(ctx.decision_time_utc).tz_convert(BANGKOK)
        return at.time() == time(7)

    def manage_position(self, ctx: StrategyContext, pos: OpenPosition) -> PositionAdvice | None:
        if not self.use_break_even:
            return None
        return break_even_advice(ctx, pos)


class TrendBoxDown(TrendBox):
    name = "trend_box_down"
    Params = TrendBoxDownParams
    direction = "DOWN"
    use_break_even = False


class TrendBoxUp(TrendBox):
    name = "trend_box_up"
    Params = TrendBoxUpParams
    direction = "UP"
    use_break_even = True
"""H4 confirmed-swing range breaks on M5 closes."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import time
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo

import pandas as pd
from pydantic import Field

from app.core.enums import Action, Side, Timeframe
from app.strategy.base_strategy import BaseStrategy, StrategyParams
from app.strategy.signal_models import (
    Condition,
    OpenPosition,
    PositionAdvice,
    ReasonCode,
    Signal,
    StrategyContext,
)
from app.strategy.trend_box import break_even_advice, in_window, running_push_exceeded, stepped
from app.strategy.zigzag import zigzag

BANGKOK = ZoneInfo("Asia/Bangkok")


class RangeBreakParams(StrategyParams):
    """Measured breakout plan constants."""

    buffer: float = Field(default=0.3, ge=0, le=2)
    tp_range: float = Field(default=0.5, gt=0, le=5)
    min_r: float = Field(default=0.8, gt=0, le=10)
    # Owner 2026-10-27: "you can take the 5 USD out". Measured first (research/.../gate_bite.py): the
    # 5.00 USD floor rejected 1 of 496 complete signals, because every target here is a swing or half an
    # H4 box. The gate stays so a lane can set one; 0.0 is off.
    min_take_profit: float = Field(default=0.0, ge=0)


class RangeBreak(BaseStrategy):
    name = "range_break"
    Params = RangeBreakParams

    def required_timeframes(self) -> tuple[Timeframe, ...]:
        return Timeframe.M1, Timeframe.M5, Timeframe.H1

    def warmup_bars(self) -> int:
        # H1 bars, not H4: the 300 H4 bars the box is read from are resampled from 1,200 closed H1 bars.
        # Declaring 300 here would let a lane configure 300 H1 bars, give the ZigZag 75 H4 bars instead of
        # 300, and change the box silently (found in review, 2026-10-27).
        return 1200

    @staticmethod
    def _h4_from_h1(h1: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
        # App frames are indexed by H1 close; the research resamples bars indexed by H1 open.
        opens = h1.copy()
        opens.index = pd.DatetimeIndex(opens.index) - pd.Timedelta(hours=1)
        return (
            opens.resample("4h").agg({"open": "first", "high": "max", "low": "min", "close": "last"}).dropna()
        )

    def evaluate(self, ctx: StrategyContext) -> Signal:
        at = pd.Timestamp(ctx.decision_time_utc)
        m5, m1 = ctx.frame(Timeframe.M5), ctx.frame(Timeframe.M1)
        h1 = ctx.frame(Timeframe.H1).iloc[-1200:]
        h4 = self._h4_from_h1(h1)
        h4 = h4[(h4.index + pd.Timedelta(hours=4)) <= at].iloc[-300:]
        if len(h4) < 300 or len(m5) < 2 or not len(m1):
            return self.hold(ctx, ReasonCode.INSUFFICIENT_DATA)
        if not in_window(at):
            return self.hold(ctx, ReasonCode.OUTSIDE_SESSION)
        swings, _forming = zigzag(h4.high.to_numpy(float), h4.low.to_numpy(float))
        highs = [s.price for s in swings if s.kind == 1]
        lows = [s.price for s in swings if s.kind == -1]
        if stepped(swings) != "FLAT" or not highs or not lows:
            return self.hold(ctx, "H4_STEPPING")
        hh, ll = highs[-1], lows[-1]
        if hh <= ll:
            return self.hold(ctx, "INVALID_H4_BOX")
        prev, price = float(m5.close.iloc[-2]), float(m5.close.iloc[-1])
        rng = hh - ll
        if price > hh >= prev:
            side, stop, target = (
                Side.BUY,
                hh - self.params.buffer * rng * 0.25,
                price + self.params.tp_range * rng,
            )
        elif price < ll <= prev:
            side, stop, target = (
                Side.SELL,
                ll + self.params.buffer * rng * 0.25,
                price - self.params.tp_range * rng,
            )
        else:
            return self.hold(ctx, "NO_CLOSE_BREAK")
        risk, reward = abs(stop - price), abs(target - price)
        if risk <= 0 or reward < self.params.min_take_profit or reward < self.params.min_r * risk:
            return self.hold(ctx, ReasonCode.RR_TOO_LOW)
        run_frame = m1[(m1.index >= at - pd.Timedelta(minutes=60)) & (m1.index < at)]
        if run_frame.empty:
            return self.hold(ctx, ReasonCode.INSUFFICIENT_DATA)
        run = float(run_frame.high.max()) - price if side is Side.BUY else price - float(run_frame.low.min())
        if running_push_exceeded(run, risk):
            return self.hold(ctx, "RUNNING_PUSH")
        return self.entry(
            ctx,
            Action.BUY if side is Side.BUY else Action.SELL,
            entry_price=price,
            stop_loss=stop,
            take_profit=target,
            conditions=(
                Condition("h4_flat", True),
                Condition("range_break", True),
                Condition("run_gate", True),
            ),
            score=80,
            explanation="confirmed H4 range break",
        )

    def should_close(self, ctx: StrategyContext, side: Side) -> bool:
        return pd.Timestamp(ctx.decision_time_utc).tz_convert(BANGKOK).time() == time(7)

    def manage_position(self, ctx: StrategyContext, pos: OpenPosition) -> PositionAdvice | None:
        return break_even_advice(ctx, pos)
"""Hand-worked tests for trend boxes: prices are deliberately small and all bar closes are UTC-aware."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta

import pandas as pd
import pytest

from app.core.enums import Action, Side, Timeframe
from app.strategy import trend_box as module
from app.strategy.signal_models import MarketContext, OpenPosition, StrategyContext
from app.strategy.trend_box import TrendBoxDown, TrendBoxUp, running_push_exceeded, stepped
from app.strategy.zigzag import Swing
from tests.unit.test_strategy_models import state

AT = datetime(2026, 10, 27, 0, 5, tzinfo=UTC)  # 07:05 Bangkok, inside Asia window


def context(*, low: float = 99.5) -> StrategyContext:
    m5idx = pd.date_range(AT - timedelta(minutes=120), AT, freq="5min", tz="UTC")
    m5 = pd.DataFrame({"open": 105.0, "high": 110.0, "low": 100.0, "close": 105.0}, index=m5idx)
    m5.iloc[-1, m5.columns.get_loc("high")] = 111.0
    m5.iloc[-1, m5.columns.get_loc("low")] = 98.0
    m5.iloc[-1, m5.columns.get_loc("close")] = 99.0
    m1idx = pd.date_range(AT - timedelta(minutes=60), AT, freq="1min", tz="UTC")
    m1 = pd.DataFrame({"high": 100.0, "low": low, "close": 99.5, "open": 99.5}, index=m1idx)
    h1idx = pd.date_range(end=AT, periods=1200, freq="1h", tz="UTC")
    h1 = pd.DataFrame({"high": 150.0, "low": 50.0, "close": 100.0, "open": 100.0}, index=h1idx)
    market = MarketContext(
        symbol="XAUUSD",
        decision_time_utc=AT,
        entry_timeframe=Timeframe.M5,
        higher_timeframe=Timeframe.H1,
        states=(state(Timeframe.M5, AT), state(Timeframe.H1, AT)),
    )
    return StrategyContext(
        market,
        {Timeframe.M1: m1, Timeframe.M5: m5, Timeframe.H1: h1},
        AT + timedelta(seconds=3),
    )


def down_swings() -> list[Swing]:
    return [Swing(10, 140.0, 1), Swing(20, 90.0, -1), Swing(30, 130.0, 1), Swing(40, 70.0, -1)]


def up_swings(retr: float = 0.5) -> list[Swing]:
    b = 2048 + retr * 2048
    return [Swing(10, 400, -1), Swing(20, b - 5, 1), Swing(30, b - 2048, -1), Swing(40, b, 1)]


def up_context() -> StrategyContext:
    ctx = context()
    m5 = ctx.frame(Timeframe.M5).copy()
    m5.iloc[:-1, m5.columns.get_loc("high")] = 2080.0
    m5.iloc[:-1, m5.columns.get_loc("low")] = 2016.0
    m5.iloc[-1, m5.columns.get_loc("high")] = 2081.0
    m5.iloc[-1, m5.columns.get_loc("low")] = 2079.0
    m5.iloc[-1, m5.columns.get_loc("close")] = 2080.1
    m1 = ctx.frame(Timeframe.M1).copy()
    m1["high"] = 2080.1
    return StrategyContext(ctx.market, {**ctx.frames, Timeframe.M5: m5, Timeframe.M1: m1}, ctx.now_utc)


def test_stepping_requires_both_swing_kinds_to_agree():
    assert stepped(down_swings()) == "DOWN"
    assert stepped([Swing(1, 100, 1), Swing(2, 70, -1), Swing(3, 110, 1), Swing(4, 80, -1)]) == "UP"
    assert stepped([Swing(1, 100, 1), Swing(2, 90, -1), Swing(3, 110, 1), Swing(4, 80, -1)]) == "FLAT"
    assert stepped([Swing(1, 140, 1), Swing(2, 70, -1), Swing(3, 130, 1), Swing(4, 80, -1)]) == "FLAT"


def test_windows_follow_bangkok_and_new_york_local_time():
    assert module.in_window(pd.Timestamp(AT))  # 07:05 Bangkok
    assert module.in_window(pd.Timestamp("2026-10-27 06:05Z"))  # 13:05 Bangkok
    assert module.in_window(pd.Timestamp("2026-10-27 12:05Z"))  # 08:05 New York, still daylight time
    assert module.in_window(pd.Timestamp("2026-11-03 13:05Z"))  # 08:05 New York after daylight time ends
    assert not module.in_window(pd.Timestamp("2026-10-27 04:00Z"))


def test_strategy_frames_are_only_m1_m5_and_h1():
    assert TrendBoxDown().required_timeframes() == (Timeframe.M1, Timeframe.M5, Timeframe.H1)
    assert TrendBoxUp().required_timeframes() == (Timeframe.M1, Timeframe.M5, Timeframe.H1)


def test_down_entry_uses_box_before_decision_bar_and_exact_plan(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "zigzag", lambda *_args: (down_swings(), Swing(41, 151, 1)))
    got = TrendBoxDown().evaluate(context())
    # The 24 prior M5 bars have top 110 / floor 100; the decision bar's 111 / 98 wick is excluded.
    assert (got.action, got.entry_price, got.stop_loss, got.take_profit) == (Action.SELL, 99.0, 112.0, 70.0)
    assert "24 bars" in next(c.detail for c in got.conditions if c.name == "box")


def test_up_entry_mirrors_trend_leg_and_stop_plan(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch):
    up = [Swing(10, 50.0, -1), Swing(20, 120.0, 1), Swing(30, 60.0, -1), Swing(40, 130.0, 1)]
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "zigzag", lambda *_args: (up, None))
    ctx = context()
    m5 = ctx.frame(Timeframe.M5).copy()
    m5.iloc[:-1, m5.columns.get_loc("high")] = 100.0
    m5.iloc[:-1, m5.columns.get_loc("low")] = 90.0
    m5.iloc[-1, m5.columns.get_loc("high")] = 102.0
    m5.iloc[-1, m5.columns.get_loc("low")] = 99.0
    m5.iloc[-1, m5.columns.get_loc("close")] = 101.0
    m1 = ctx.frame(Timeframe.M1).copy()
    m1["high"] = 101.0
    ctx = StrategyContext(ctx.market, {**ctx.frames, Timeframe.M5: m5, Timeframe.M1: m1}, ctx.now_utc)
    got = TrendBoxUp().evaluate(ctx)
    # Leg 60->130 (70 USD), box 100/90, entry 101; SL=90-2=88 and target=130.
    assert (got.action, got.entry_price, got.stop_loss, got.take_profit) == (Action.BUY, 101.0, 88.0, 130.0)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("retr", "should_enter"),
    [(0.382, True), (0.786, True), (0.381, False), (0.787, False)],
)
def test_up_retrace_bounds_are_inclusive(retr: float, should_enter: bool, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "zigzag", lambda *_args: (up_swings(retr), None))
    assert TrendBoxUp().evaluate(up_context()).action.is_entry is should_enter


def test_up_box_and_close_trigger_gates(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "zigzag", lambda *_args: (up_swings(), None))
    ctx = up_context()
    m5 = ctx.frame(Timeframe.M5).copy()
    m5.iloc[-1, m5.columns.get_loc("close")] = 2079.9  # The wick is above top; the close is not.
    wick_only = StrategyContext(ctx.market, {**ctx.frames, Timeframe.M5: m5}, ctx.now_utc)
    assert TrendBoxUp().evaluate(wick_only).action is Action.HOLD
    m5.iloc[:-1, m5.columns.get_loc("high")] = 2800.0  # 784 USD > .33 x 2,048 USD leg.
    too_tall = StrategyContext(ctx.market, {**ctx.frames, Timeframe.M5: m5}, ctx.now_utc)
    assert TrendBoxUp().evaluate(too_tall).action is Action.HOLD


def test_up_run_and_reward_risk_gates_reject(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "zigzag", lambda *_args: (up_swings(), None))
    ctx = up_context()
    m1 = ctx.frame(Timeframe.M1).copy()
    m1["high"] = 2180.0  # Run 99.9 USD > 1.2 x the 76.9 USD initial risk.
    running = StrategyContext(ctx.market, {**ctx.frames, Timeframe.M1: m1}, ctx.now_utc)
    assert TrendBoxUp().evaluate(running).action is Action.HOLD
    m5 = ctx.frame(Timeframe.M5).copy()
    m5.iloc[-1, m5.columns.get_loc("close")] = 3071.0  # Reward 1, risk 1,067.8: below 1.2 R.
    m1["high"] = 3071.0
    weak = StrategyContext(ctx.market, {**ctx.frames, Timeframe.M5: m5, Timeframe.M1: m1}, ctx.now_utc)
    assert TrendBoxUp().evaluate(weak).action is Action.HOLD


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("retr", "should_enter"),
    [(0.382, True), (0.786, True), (0.381, False), (0.787, False)],
)
def test_retrace_bounds_are_inclusive(retr: float, should_enter: bool, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch):
    # For top/floor 105/104, choose B so (104.5-B)/(120-B) equals the tested retrace.
    b = (104.5 - 120 * retr) / (1 - retr)
    swings = [Swing(10, 140, 1), Swing(20, b + 5, -1), Swing(30, 120, 1), Swing(40, b, -1)]
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "zigzag", lambda *_args: (swings, None))
    ctx = context()
    m5 = ctx.frame(Timeframe.M5).copy()
    m5.iloc[:-1, m5.columns.get_loc("high")] = 105.0
    m5.iloc[:-1, m5.columns.get_loc("low")] = 104.0
    m5.iloc[-1, m5.columns.get_loc("close")] = 103.9
    m1 = ctx.frame(Timeframe.M1).copy()
    m1["low"] = 103.9
    ctx = StrategyContext(ctx.market, {**ctx.frames, Timeframe.M5: m5, Timeframe.M1: m1}, ctx.now_utc)
    got = TrendBoxDown().evaluate(ctx)
    assert got.action.is_entry is should_enter


def test_wick_only_break_and_running_push_hold(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "zigzag", lambda *_args: (down_swings(), None))
    ctx = context()
    frame = ctx.frame(Timeframe.M5).copy()
    frame.iloc[-1, frame.columns.get_loc("close")] = 101.0  # wick low remains 98, close is inside box
    no_close = StrategyContext(ctx.market, {**ctx.frames, Timeframe.M5: frame}, ctx.now_utc)
    assert TrendBoxDown().evaluate(no_close).action is Action.HOLD
    assert TrendBoxDown().evaluate(context(low=80.0)).action is Action.HOLD  # 19 USD run > 1.2 x 13 risk


def test_run_threshold_is_strict_and_uses_the_preceding_sixty_minutes(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch):
    assert not running_push_exceeded(6.0, 5.0)
    assert running_push_exceeded(6.01, 5.0)
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "zigzag", lambda *_args: (down_swings(), None))
    ctx = context()
    m1 = ctx.frame(Timeframe.M1).copy()
    m1["low"] = 83.4  # 99 - 83.4 = 15.6 = 1.2 x the 13 USD risk: equality passes.
    m1.iloc[-1, m1.columns.get_loc("low")] = 1.0  # at the decision timestamp; excluded from the run window
    ctx = StrategyContext(ctx.market, {**ctx.frames, Timeframe.M1: m1}, ctx.now_utc)
    assert TrendBoxDown().evaluate(ctx).action.is_entry
    m1.iloc[0, m1.columns.get_loc("low")] = 83.39  # exactly 60 minutes old; included
    just_over = StrategyContext(ctx.market, {**ctx.frames, Timeframe.M1: m1}, ctx.now_utc)
    assert TrendBoxDown().evaluate(just_over).action is Action.HOLD


def test_too_tall_box_and_close_equal_to_floor_hold(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "zigzag", lambda *_args: (down_swings(), None))
    ctx = context()
    m5 = ctx.frame(Timeframe.M5).copy()
    m5.iloc[:-1, m5.columns.get_loc("high")] = 120.0  # 20 USD > .2 x 60 USD leg
    tall = StrategyContext(ctx.market, {**ctx.frames, Timeframe.M5: m5}, ctx.now_utc)
    assert TrendBoxDown().evaluate(tall).action is Action.HOLD
    m5.iloc[:-1, m5.columns.get_loc("high")] = 110.0
    m5.iloc[-1, m5.columns.get_loc("close")] = 100.0  # close is at, not below, floor
    equal_floor = StrategyContext(ctx.market, {**ctx.frames, Timeframe.M5: m5}, ctx.now_utc)
    assert TrendBoxDown().evaluate(equal_floor).action is Action.HOLD


def test_weak_reward_to_risk_holds(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "zigzag", lambda *_args: (down_swings(), None))
    ctx = context()
    m5 = ctx.frame(Timeframe.M5).copy()
    m5.iloc[-1, m5.columns.get_loc("close")] = 80.0
    m1 = ctx.frame(Timeframe.M1).copy()
    m1["low"] = 80.0  # No running push; only the reward/risk gate can reject this candidate.
    rejected = StrategyContext(ctx.market, {**ctx.frames, Timeframe.M5: m5, Timeframe.M1: m1}, ctx.now_utc)
    assert TrendBoxDown().evaluate(rejected).action is Action.HOLD


def test_longest_fitting_box_is_not_replaced_after_retrace_failure(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch):
    # The 24-bar box is 117/105, exactly 12 USD tall; its retrace is .85 and must stop the search.
    # Removing its oldest bar leaves a 106/105 box with a valid .758 retrace that must not be tried.
    swings = [Swing(10, 140, 1), Swing(20, 90, -1), Swing(30, 120, 1), Swing(40, 60, -1)]
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "zigzag", lambda *_args: (swings, None))
    ctx = context()
    m5 = ctx.frame(Timeframe.M5).copy()
    m5.iloc[:-1, m5.columns.get_loc("high")] = 106.0
    m5.iloc[:-1, m5.columns.get_loc("low")] = 105.0
    m5.iloc[0, m5.columns.get_loc("high")] = 117.0
    m5.iloc[0, m5.columns.get_loc("low")] = 105.0
    m5.iloc[-1, m5.columns.get_loc("close")] = 104.0
    m1 = ctx.frame(Timeframe.M1).copy()
    m1["low"] = 104.0
    ctx = StrategyContext(ctx.market, {**ctx.frames, Timeframe.M5: m5, Timeframe.M1: m1}, ctx.now_utc)
    assert TrendBoxDown().evaluate(ctx).action is Action.HOLD


def test_up_break_even_waits_for_touch_then_moves_to_entry():
    strat = TrendBoxUp()
    pos = OpenPosition(Side.BUY, 100, 90, 90, 120, AT - timedelta(minutes=10))
    ctx = context()
    m1 = ctx.frame(Timeframe.M1).copy()
    m1["high"] = 104.9
    before = StrategyContext(ctx.market, {**ctx.frames, Timeframe.M1: m1}, ctx.now_utc)
    assert strat.manage_position(before, pos) is None
    m1.iloc[-1, m1.columns.get_loc("high")] = 105.0
    reached = StrategyContext(ctx.market, {**ctx.frames, Timeframe.M1: m1}, ctx.now_utc)
    advice = strat.manage_position(reached, pos)
    assert advice is not None and advice.new_sl == 100


def test_original_stop_is_checked_before_same_bar_break_even_touch():
    strat = TrendBoxUp()
    pos = OpenPosition(Side.BUY, 100, 90, 90, 120, AT - timedelta(minutes=10))
    ctx = context()
    m1 = ctx.frame(Timeframe.M1).copy()
    m1.iloc[-1, m1.columns.get_loc("high")] = 105.0
    m1.iloc[-1, m1.columns.get_loc("low")] = 90.0
    same_bar = StrategyContext(ctx.market, {**ctx.frames, Timeframe.M1: m1}, ctx.now_utc)
    assert strat.manage_position(same_bar, pos) is None


def test_break_even_never_replaces_a_better_stop():
    strat = TrendBoxUp()
    pos = OpenPosition(Side.BUY, 100, 90, 101, 120, AT - timedelta(minutes=10))
    ctx = context()
    m1 = ctx.frame(Timeframe.M1).copy()
    m1["high"], m1["low"] = 105.0, 102.0
    advanced = StrategyContext(ctx.market, {**ctx.frames, Timeframe.M1: m1}, ctx.now_utc)
    assert strat.manage_position(advanced, pos) is None


def test_daily_flat_is_at_seven_thai():
    strat = TrendBoxDown()
    at = datetime(2026, 10, 27, 0, 0, tzinfo=UTC)
    market = MarketContext(
        symbol="XAUUSD",
        decision_time_utc=at,
        entry_timeframe=Timeframe.M5,
        higher_timeframe=Timeframe.H1,
        states=(state(Timeframe.M5, at), state(Timeframe.H1, at)),
    )
    ctx = StrategyContext(market, {}, at + timedelta(seconds=3))
    assert strat.should_close(ctx, Side.SELL)


def test_a_box_taller_than_the_cap_is_not_a_box(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch):
    # Found in review 2026-10-27: removing the `box * leg` cap left every test green. The leg is 130 -> 70, so
    # 60; the cap is 0.2 x 60 = 12. Widen the 24 bars before the decision to 110 - 97 = 13 and no window fits,
    # while the decision bar still closes under the floor - so only the cap can be refusing.
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "zigzag", lambda *_args: (down_swings(), Swing(41, 151, 1)))
    ctx = context()
    m5 = ctx.frame(Timeframe.M5).copy()
    m5.iloc[:-1, m5.columns.get_loc("low")] = 97.0
    m5.iloc[-1, m5.columns.get_loc("close")] = 96.0  # through the 97 floor: the trigger is satisfied
    m5.iloc[-1, m5.columns.get_loc("low")] = 95.0
    wide = StrategyContext(ctx.market, {**ctx.frames, Timeframe.M5: m5}, ctx.now_utc)
    assert TrendBoxDown().evaluate(wide).action is Action.HOLD
    # the same bars with the cap raised to 0.3 x 60 = 18 do enter, so it was the cap that refused
    got = TrendBoxDown(TrendBoxDown.Params(box=0.3)).evaluate(wide)
    assert (got.action, got.entry_price, got.take_profit) == (Action.SELL, 96.0, 70.0)
    assert got.stop_loss == pytest.approx(110 + 0.2 * 13)
"""Hand-worked H4 box break cases for the range strategy."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import replace
from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta

import pandas as pd
import pytest

from app.core.enums import Action, Side, Timeframe
from app.strategy import range_break as module
from app.strategy.range_break import RangeBreak
from app.strategy.signal_models import MarketContext, OpenPosition, StrategyContext
from app.strategy.zigzag import Swing
from tests.unit.test_strategy_models import state

AT = datetime(2026, 10, 27, 0, 5, tzinfo=UTC)


def context(*, prev: float = 100.0, price: float = 101.0, high: float = 101.0, low: float = 99.0):
    m5idx = pd.date_range(end=AT, periods=2, freq="5min", tz="UTC")
    m5 = pd.DataFrame(
        {"open": [prev, price], "high": [prev, high], "low": [prev, low], "close": [prev, price]},
        index=m5idx,
    )
    h1idx = pd.date_range(end=datetime(2026, 10, 27, 0, 0, tzinfo=UTC), periods=1200, freq="1h")
    h1 = pd.DataFrame({"open": 90.0, "high": 100.0, "low": 80.0, "close": 90.0}, index=h1idx)
    m1idx = pd.date_range(AT - timedelta(minutes=60), AT, freq="1min", tz="UTC")
    m1 = pd.DataFrame({"open": 100.0, "high": high, "low": low, "close": price}, index=m1idx)
    market = MarketContext(
        symbol="XAUUSD",
        decision_time_utc=AT,
        entry_timeframe=Timeframe.M5,
        higher_timeframe=Timeframe.H1,
        states=(state(Timeframe.M5, AT), state(Timeframe.H1, AT)),
    )
    return StrategyContext(
        market,
        {Timeframe.M1: m1, Timeframe.M5: m5, Timeframe.H1: h1},
        AT + timedelta(seconds=3),
    )


def flat_swings() -> list[Swing]:
    return [Swing(1, 105, 1), Swing(2, 75, -1), Swing(3, 100, 1), Swing(4, 80, -1)]


def test_break_plan_matches_the_calculated_h4_box(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "zigzag", lambda *_args: (flat_swings(), Swing(5, 110, 1)))
    got = RangeBreak().evaluate(context())
    # H4 top/floor = 100/80, so rng=20, stop=100 - .3*20*.25=98.5, TP=101 + .5*20=111.
    assert (got.action, got.entry_price, got.stop_loss, got.take_profit) == (Action.BUY, 101.0, 98.5, 111.0)


def test_h4_flat_box_needs_only_one_confirmed_swing_per_side(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "zigzag", lambda *_args: ([Swing(1, 100, 1), Swing(2, 80, -1)], None))
    assert RangeBreak().evaluate(context()).action is Action.BUY


def test_trigger_needs_the_close_to_leave_the_box(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "zigzag", lambda *_args: (flat_swings(), None))
    assert RangeBreak().evaluate(context(price=99.5, high=101, low=98)).action is Action.HOLD
    assert RangeBreak().evaluate(context(prev=101, price=101)).action is Action.HOLD


def test_h4_stepping_and_run_gate_reject(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch):
    up = [Swing(1, 90, 1), Swing(2, 70, -1), Swing(3, 100, 1), Swing(4, 80, -1)]
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "zigzag", lambda *_args: (up, None))
    assert RangeBreak().evaluate(context()).action is Action.HOLD
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "zigzag", lambda *_args: (flat_swings(), None))
    assert RangeBreak().evaluate(context(high=120)).action is Action.HOLD


def test_range_strategy_uses_only_m1_m5_and_h1_frames():
    assert RangeBreak().required_timeframes() == (Timeframe.M1, Timeframe.M5, Timeframe.H1)


def test_range_reward_must_cover_minimum_risk_multiple(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "zigzag", lambda *_args: (flat_swings(), None))
    # Close at 120 after crossing 100: reward=10 but risk=21.5, below the required 0.8 R.
    assert RangeBreak().evaluate(context(price=120, high=120, low=119)).action is Action.HOLD


def test_sell_break_plan_is_the_mirror_and_reward_floor_is_enforced(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch):
    down = [Swing(1, 95, 1), Swing(2, 65, -1), Swing(3, 90, 1), Swing(4, 78, -1)]
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "zigzag", lambda *_args: (down, None))
    got = RangeBreak().evaluate(context(prev=78, price=77, high=78, low=76))
    # The box is 90/78 (range 12): stop 78 + .3*12*.25 = 78.9; target 77 - .5*12 = 71.
    assert (got.action, got.entry_price, got.stop_loss, got.take_profit) == (Action.SELL, 77.0, 78.9, 71.0)
    small_box = [Swing(1, 95, 1), Swing(2, 75, -1), Swing(3, 90, 1), Swing(4, 82, -1)]
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "zigzag", lambda *_args: (small_box, None))
    # the box is 90/82: a BUY at 91 targets 95, so a reward of 4.00 USD. It passes with the floor off
    # (the default since 2026-10-27) and is refused by a lane that sets one.
    bars = context(prev=90, price=91, high=91, low=90)
    assert RangeBreak().evaluate(bars).action is Action.BUY
    floored = RangeBreak(RangeBreak.Params(min_take_profit=5.0)).evaluate(bars)
    assert floored.action is Action.HOLD


def test_break_even_requires_half_r_touch_then_returns_entry_stop():
    strat = RangeBreak()
    pos = OpenPosition(Side.BUY, 100, 90, 90, 120, AT - timedelta(minutes=10))
    ctx = context(high=104.9, low=101)
    assert strat.manage_position(ctx, pos) is None
    reached = context(high=105, low=101)
    advice = strat.manage_position(reached, pos)
    assert advice is not None and advice.new_sl == 100


def test_h4_resampling_uses_the_h1_four_hour_boundaries():
    idx = pd.DatetimeIndex(
        [
            "2026-10-26 17:00Z",
            "2026-10-26 18:00Z",
            "2026-10-26 19:00Z",
            "2026-10-27 01:00Z",
            "2026-10-27 02:00Z",
        ]
    )
    frame = pd.DataFrame({"open": 1, "high": 2, "low": 0, "close": 1}, index=idx)
    h4 = RangeBreak._h4_from_h1(frame)
    # Shift close times to H1 opens: 20:00 has no H1 data and drops; nonempty partial H4 bins remain.
    assert list(h4.index) == list(pd.to_datetime(["2026-10-26 16:00Z", "2026-10-27 00:00Z"]))


def test_h4_is_built_from_h1_open_times_and_only_from_closed_bars(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch):
    # Found in review 2026-10-27: dropping the close-to-open shift, or letting an unclosed H4 bar in, left
    # every test green. App H1 frames are stamped at the close, so an H1 bar stamped 04:00 covers 03:00-04:00
    # and belongs to the 00:00-04:00 H4 bar, not to 04:00-08:00.
    idx = pd.date_range("2026-10-26 01:00", periods=8, freq="1h", tz="UTC")  # closes 01:00 .. 08:00
    h1 = pd.DataFrame({"open": 100.0, "high": [101.0, 102, 103, 104, 105, 106, 107, 108],
                       "low": 99.0, "close": 100.0}, index=idx)  # fmt: skip
    h4 = RangeBreak._h4_from_h1(h1)
    # the shift puts the closes 01:00-04:00 into the 00:00 bar (high 104) and 05:00-08:00 into 04:00 (high 108)
    assert list(h4.index) == [
        pd.Timestamp("2026-10-26 00:00", tz="UTC"),
        pd.Timestamp("2026-10-26 04:00", tz="UTC"),
    ]
    assert list(h4.high) == [104.0, 108.0]


def test_an_unclosed_h4_bar_never_reaches_the_zigzag(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch):
    # The partner of the test above, through evaluate() instead of a copy of the filter: 1,200 H1 bars whose
    # closes end at 01:00 leave the 00:00-04:00 H4 bar still forming at the 01:05 decision (08:05 Bangkok,
    # inside the Asia window). Its one H1 bar carries a high of 999, so reading the forming bar shows up.
    ctx = context()
    closes = pd.date_range(end=datetime(2026, 10, 27, 1, 0, tzinfo=UTC), periods=1200, freq="1h")
    highs = [100.0] * 1199 + [999.0]  # the H1 bar closing 01:00: the only one in the forming H4 bar
    h1 = pd.DataFrame({"open": 90.0, "high": highs, "low": 80.0, "close": 90.0}, index=closes)
    at = datetime(2026, 10, 27, 1, 5, tzinfo=UTC)
    m5 = ctx.frame(Timeframe.M5).copy()
    m5.index = pd.DatetimeIndex([at - timedelta(minutes=5), at])
    m1 = ctx.frame(Timeframe.M1).copy()
    m1.index = pd.date_range(at - timedelta(minutes=60), at, freq="1min", tz="UTC")
    market = replace(ctx.market, decision_time_utc=at)
    later = StrategyContext(
        market, {Timeframe.M1: m1, Timeframe.M5: m5, Timeframe.H1: h1}, at + timedelta(seconds=3)
    )
    seen: list[float] = []

    def capture(high, low):
        seen.extend(float(x) for x in high)
        return flat_swings(), None

    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "zigzag", capture)
    RangeBreak().evaluate(later)
    assert seen and 999.0 not in seen
    assert len(seen) == 300
"""The research session's own planted bugs against the delivered strategies (DEV_SPEC.md section 4).

Deliberately NOT the ones the developer session wrote: these are chosen from the spec's own wording, to test
whether its tests catch mistakes it did not think of. Each bug is written in, the developer's own tests are
run, and the file is restored - with a crash-safe backup, so a killed run cannot leave a bug behind.

    .venv\\Scripts\\python <this file>
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import subprocess  # nosec B404 - runs this repo's own pytest
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(r"C:\Users\korap\taa")
BACKUP = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / ".my_bugs_backup"
TESTS = ["tests/unit/test_strategy_trend_box.py", "tests/unit/test_strategy_range_break.py"]
TB, RB = "app/strategy/trend_box.py", "app/strategy/range_break.py"

BUGS: list[tuple[str, str, str, str]] = [
    # the spec's own sentences, each broken one way
    ("3.1.1 the New York window is fixed to a clock hour instead of New York local time",
     TB, 'ZoneInfo("America/New_York")', 'ZoneInfo("UTC")'),
    ("3.1.3 the run is measured over 30 minutes, not 60", TB, "minutes=60", "minutes=30"),
    ("3.1.3 the run gate is ten times looser than the measured 1.2 R",
     TB, "return run > 1.2 * risk", "return run > 12.0 * risk"),
    ("3.2.3 the box may be as tall as the whole leg", TB, "self.params.box * leg", "leg"),
    ("3.2.4 the retrace is measured from A, not from B", TB, "- b.price) / leg", "- a.price) / leg"),
    ("3.2.6 the take-profit is the leg's start, not the swing it runs to", TB, "target = b.price", "target = a.price"),
    ("3.2.6 the stop sits on the box edge with no buffer", TB,
     "top + self.params.buffer * (top - floor) if down else floor - self.params.buffer * (top - floor)",
     "top if down else floor"),
    ("3.3.1 a trending H4 also qualifies as a range", RB, 'stepped(swings) != "FLAT" or ', ""),
    ("3.3.3 the break is taken without the previous close being inside",
     RB, "price > hh >= prev", "price > hh"),
    ("3.3.4 a BUY's take-profit is the whole box, not tp_range of it",
     RB, "price + self.params.tp_range * rng,", "price + rng,"),
    ("3.4 break-even moves the stop even before +0.5 R", TB, "ahead >= 0.5 * risk", "ahead >= 0.0 * risk"),
    ("H4 is resampled without the close-to-open shift", RB, '- pd.Timedelta(hours=1)', "- pd.Timedelta(hours=0)"),
    ("H4 uses bars that have not closed yet", RB, "(h4.index + pd.Timedelta(hours=4)) <= at", "h4.index <= at"),
    ("the ZigZag's forming point is used as a confirmed swing", RB,
     "swings, _forming = zigzag(h4.high.to_numpy(float), h4.low.to_numpy(float))",
     ("swings, _forming = zigzag(h4.high.to_numpy(float), h4.low.to_numpy(float))\n"
      "        swings = swings + ([_forming] if _forming is not None else [])")),
]  # fmt: skip


def run() -> bool:
    out = subprocess.run(  # nosec B603 - fixed argv
        [sys.executable, "-m", "pytest", "-q", "-x", "-p", "no:cacheprovider", *TESTS],
        cwd=ROOT, capture_output=True, text=True, check=False,
    )  # fmt: skip
    return out.returncode == 0


def restore_any() -> None:
    for f in sorted(BACKUP.glob("*.bak")) if BACKUP.exists() else []:
        head, body = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8").split("\n", 1)
        (ROOT / head).write_text(body, encoding="utf-8")
        print(f"restored {head} from an interrupted run")
        f.unlink()


def main() -> int:
    sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
    restore_any()
    if not run():
        print("the developer's tests do not pass on the clean source - stopping")
        return 1
    missed = []
    for name, path, old, new in BUGS:
        f = ROOT / path
        src = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        if src.count(old) != 1 or old == new:
            print(f"SKIP    {name} (the text is not unique in {path}, or the bug is a no-op)")
            continue
        BACKUP.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        parked = BACKUP / (path.replace("/", "_") + ".bak")
        parked.write_text(path + "\n" + src, encoding="utf-8")
        try:
            f.write_text(src.replace(old, new), encoding="utf-8")
            caught = not run()
        finally:
            f.write_text(src, encoding="utf-8")
            parked.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        print(f"{'caught' if caught else 'MISSED'}  {name}")
        if not caught:
            missed.append(name)
    print(f"\n{len(BUGS) - len(missed)}/{len(BUGS)} caught by the developer's own tests")
    for m in missed:
        print(f"  MISSED: {m}")
    return 1 if missed else 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

Run with ``.venv\\Scripts\\python research\\2026-10-26-three-bots\\plant_bugs.py``. The committed output is

def run(test: str, test_name: str) -> bool:
    result = subprocess.run(  # noqa: S603 - fixed repo-owned test command
        [sys.executable, "-m", "pytest", "-q", "-p", "no:cacheprovider", f"{test}::{test_name}"],
        cwd=ROOT,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        check=False,
    )  # nosec B603 - fixed arguments run a repository test
    return result.returncode == 0


def main() -> int:
    baseline = subprocess.run(  # noqa: S603 - fixed repo-owned test command
        [sys.executable, "-m", "pytest", "-q", "-p", "no:cacheprovider", TEST_TREND, TEST_RANGE],
        cwd=ROOT,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        check=False,
    )
    if baseline.returncode:
        raise RuntimeError(f"clean strategy tests failed:\n{baseline.stdout}\n{baseline.stderr}")
    changed: dict[Path, str] = {}
    results: list[str] = []
    try:
        for name, relpath, original, replacement, test_name in BUGS:
            target = ROOT / relpath
            clean = target.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
            if original not in clean:
                raise RuntimeError(f"mutation anchor missing for: {name}")
            changed[target] = clean
            target.write_text(clean.replace(original, replacement, 1), encoding="utf-8")
            caught = not run(TEST_TREND if relpath == TREND else TEST_RANGE, test_name)
            results.append(f"{'CAUGHT' if caught else 'MISSED'} | {name}")
            target.write_text(clean, encoding="utf-8")
            changed.pop(target)
    finally:
        for target, clean in changed.items():
            target.write_text(clean, encoding="utf-8")
    output = "\n".join([f"Planted bugs: {len(BUGS)}", *results]) + "\n"
    destination = HERE / "plant_bugs_output.txt"
    destination.write_text(output, encoding="utf-8")
    sys.stdout.write(output)
    return 1 if any(line.startswith("MISSED") for line in results) else 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

      def recheck_plan_stops(self, symbol: str, stops: dict[str, float | None]) -> None:
          """The plan's orders against the map's stops now (C4 at the fill, not only when the plan was written -
          07-08 13:16 filled 16 short of the zone): a stop short of the map's is moved out to it; an order whose
>         take-profit is then under ``min_take_profit`` or its R under ``plan_min_rr`` is withdrawn."""

          keep = []

              self._plan[symbol] = keep

              return "keep" if short else "fill"

          return "keep"

              reach = abs(o.target - price)
>             keep = max(reach * (1 - p.target_trim), min(p.min_take_profit, reach))

"""The AI trader as a lane strategy (PLAN Â§A37, TAA-1806): its own name, magic number, statistics and Trade
history like any strategy. It never asks the AI itself (strategy code is pure): the engine hands it the AI's
decisions (:meth:`AITrader.accept`, :meth:`AITrader.request_exit`) and feeds it quotes (:meth:`on_quote`), the
path of the release straddle. Every entry it makes is an ordinary signal: the decision engine sizes it by the
owner's profile (the AI's 'half' halves the risk) and runs every check.

An order fills at the quote once price is at the AI's entry (within ``market_tolerance``): a sell when the bid
is at or above it, a buy when the ask is at or below it. It is dropped when its stop is touched first, after
``pending_minutes``, or when a new decision replaces it.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses
from collections.abc import Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime

import pandas as pd
from pydantic import Field

from app.core.clock import ensure_utc
from app.core.enums import Action, ExitReason, Side, Timeframe
from app.strategy import confirm as cf
from app.strategy.base_strategy import BaseStrategy, StrategyParams
from app.strategy.signal_models import (
    Condition,
    OpenPosition,
    PositionAdvice,
    ReasonCode,
    Signal,
    StrategyContext,
)

Held = tuple[Side, float, float | None, float | None, datetime]  # side, entry, stop, target, opened


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AIOrder:
    """One AI decision to enter, as the engine hands it over."""

    side: Side
    entry: float
    stop: float
    target: float | None
    risk_scale: float  # 1.0 for 'full', 0.5 for 'half'
    setup: str
    reasons: tuple[str, ...]
    kb_ids: tuple[str, ...]
    confidence: int
    decided_at: datetime
    valid_until: datetime
    key: str  # the decision's id (its stored prompt and answer)
    watch_above: float | None = None  # the prices the AI asked to be woken at
    watch_below: float | None = None
    # 'market' (fill now, within market_tolerance), 'limit' (a sell above / a buy below the price: fill when
    # price comes to it), 'stop' (a sell below / a buy above the price: fill when price breaks to it); None:
    # read from the entry against the first quote (owner 2026-10-25: "a limit or a stop order, as the
    # situation needs, with an expiry")
    kind: str | None = None
    exit_level: float | None = None  # C6: 3 M5 closes back across it end the trade early (None: the entry)
    # a BUY / SELL decision (the AI read the closed bar and enters now): only such an order may be a market
    # order; a WAIT's order and a plan order are limit / stop orders (07-08 13:15: a wait order at 4,132.50
    # was read as market and filled 0.95 worse)
    direct: bool = False

    def geometry_ok(self, price: float) -> bool:
        """The stop beyond the price on the losing side, the target (if any) on the winning side."""
        if self.side is Side.SELL:
            return self.stop > price and (self.target is None or self.target < price)
        return self.stop < price and (self.target is None or self.target > price)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AIEntry:
    order: AIOrder
    at: datetime
    price: float

    @property
    def side(self) -> Side:
        return self.order.side


class AITraderParams(StrategyParams):
    pending_minutes: int = Field(default=30, gt=0, le=240)  # how long an order waits for its price
    market_tolerance: float = Field(
        default=1.0, ge=0, le=50
    )  # price units: this close to the entry fills now
    # the take-profit this share of the way short of the AI's target (owner 2026-10-24: 5-10 % before it; the
    # 2026-07-07 replay's 12:04 sell came within 0.6 of an 11.4 target, then lost)
    target_trim: float = Field(default=0.10, ge=0, lt=1)
    # the trim never brings the take-profit nearer than this to the entry (price units; owner 2026-10-25, C5)
    min_take_profit: float = Field(default=0.0, ge=0)
    # the AI's exit before the stop / take-profit stands only after this many M5 closes back across its level
    # (C6's failed break) or this long held (the scalp's time stop) - 07-07, ISSUES 28
    exit_m5_closes: int = Field(default=3, ge=1, le=12)
    exit_minutes: int = Field(default=60, ge=5, le=480)
    plan_min_rr: float = Field(default=0.2, ge=0)  # a plan order moved out to the map's stop keeps this R
    # C9 (P24): no new trade on a side this long after a trade of that side reached its take-profit
    tp_block_minutes: int = Field(default=30, ge=0, le=240)
    # owner 2026-10-26: "wait for the chart to confirm before entering; never enter at the first moment
    # price gets there"; "a signal at the level - a chart pattern or a candlestick pattern on a larger
    # timeframe") - a limit / stop order whose price is reached is armed, and fills only after a signal
    # at its level on M5 / M15 (app/strategy/confirm.py), at a price no further than chase_share of its
    # stop distance past the entry
    confirm_entries: bool = True
    chase_share: float = Field(default=0.5, gt=0, le=2)
    # no fill into a push still running toward the stop (owner 2026-10-26: "you analysed it so long - why not
    # fix it"): when price ran toward the stop by more than run_share x the stop distance in the last
    # run_minutes, the order waits until that is no longer so. 06-09 Jul: every trade entered after a run of
    # 1.3x or more lost (7 of 7), the winners came after 1.2x or less (losers.py); 0 turns it off
    run_share: float = Field(default=1.2, ge=0, le=10)
    run_minutes: int = Field(default=60, ge=5, le=240)


def m5_closes(m1: pd.DataFrame | None, count: int) -> list[float]:
    """The last ``count`` complete M5 closes from closed M1 bars indexed by their close time."""
    if m1 is None or not len(m1):
        return []
    closes = m1["close"].resample("5min", label="right", closed="right").last().dropna()
    last = pd.Timestamp(m1.index[-1])
    if closes.index[-1] > last:  # the bucket still forming
        closes = closes.iloc[:-1]
    return [float(x) for x in closes.iloc[-count:]]


def exit_allowed(
    m1: pd.DataFrame | None,
    pos: OpenPosition,
    now: datetime,
    p: AITraderParams,
    level: float | None = None,
) -> str | None:
    """Why the AI's exit stands, or None: C6 (owner 2026-10-23: "a failed break - 3 M5 closes back inside the
    range") - ``exit_m5_closes`` M5 closes back across ``level``, the level the trade is built on (the range
    edge it broke; None: the entry - 07-08 14:46 used the entry and let out a sell the range kept) - or the
    time stop (``exit_minutes`` held)."""
    held = (ensure_utc(now) - ensure_utc(pos.entry_time)).total_seconds() / 60
    if held >= p.exit_minutes:
        return f"the time stop, {held:.0f} min held"
    mark = pos.entry if level is None else level
    closes = m5_closes(m1, p.exit_m5_closes)
    against = [c > mark if pos.side is Side.SELL else c < mark for c in closes]
    if len(against) == p.exit_m5_closes and all(against):
        return f"{p.exit_m5_closes} M5 closes back across {mark:.2f} (C6)"
    return None


class AITrader(BaseStrategy):
    name = "ai_trader"
    description = (
        "The AI trader (PLAN Â§A37): the engine hands it the AI's decisions; it enters on quotes. Unproven."
    )
    Params = AITraderParams
    advised = False

    def __init__(self, params: StrategyParams | None = None) -> None:
        super().__init__(params)
        self._orders: dict[str, AIOrder] = {}
        self._plan: dict[str, list[AIOrder]] = {}  # the day plan's orders, placed when the plan is written
        self._kinds: dict[str, str] = {}  # an order's kind read at its first quote (by its key)
        self._levels: dict[str, float | None] = {}  # the open trade's C6 level (from its order)
        self._blocked: dict[tuple[str, Side], datetime] = {}  # no fill on a side until (C9 after a TP)
        self._held: dict[str, list[Held]] = {}  # the positions seen at the last note_positions
        self._touched: dict[str, datetime] = {}  # an order's price first reached (by its key): armed
        self._confirmed: dict[str, str] = {}  # armed orders a signal at the level confirmed (key: signal)
        self._m1: dict[
            str, pd.DataFrame
        ] = {}  # the closed M1 bars of the last confirm_orders (the run check)
        self._exits: set[str] = set()

    def required_timeframes(self) -> tuple[Timeframe, ...]:
        return (Timeframe.M1,)

    def warmup_bars(self) -> int:
        return 50

    def evaluate(self, ctx: StrategyContext) -> Signal:
        return self.hold(ctx, ReasonCode.NO_SETUP, explanation="the AI trader enters on quotes, not on bars")

    # --- the engine's hand-over ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def accept(self, symbol: str, order: AIOrder) -> None:
        """A new AI order for ``symbol``; it replaces a waiting one, and an exit asked earlier no longer
        applies (it would close the new position at its first management)."""
        self._orders[symbol] = order
        self._exits.discard(symbol)

    def cancel(self, symbol: str) -> None:
        self._orders.pop(symbol, None)

    def request_exit(self, symbol: str) -> None:
        """The AI asked to close this lane's open position on ``symbol`` (applied at its next management)."""
        self._exits.add(symbol)

    def set_plan_orders(self, symbol: str, orders: list[AIOrder]) -> None:
        """The day plan's orders for ``symbol`` (owner 2026-10-25: a trader places the plan's orders in
        advance); they replace the previous window's. The first to fill withdraws the others."""
        self._plan[symbol] = list(orders)

    def plan_orders(self, symbol: str) -> list[AIOrder]:
        return list(self._plan.get(symbol, []))

    def pending(self, symbol: str, at: datetime | None = None) -> AIOrder | None:
        """The AI's waiting order; with ``at``, an expired one is dropped first (07-06 22:37: an order expired
        at 20:53 still showed as waiting, so the AI was woken outside the windows all night)."""
        order = self._orders.get(symbol)
        if order is not None and at is not None and ensure_utc(at) > order.valid_until:
            self._orders.pop(symbol)
            return None
        return order

    def block_side(self, symbol: str, side: Side, until: datetime) -> None:
        """No new fill on ``side`` until ``until`` (C9 / P24: the cycle that just reached its take-profit is
        over - 07-08 08:16 bought the 08:11 sweep again three minutes after its take-profit)."""
        self._blocked[(symbol, side)] = ensure_utc(until)

    def note_positions(
        self, symbol: str, held: Sequence[Held], m1: pd.DataFrame | None
    ) -> list[tuple[Side, datetime]]:
        """The positions now (side, entry, stop, target, opened) against the last ones: a position gone whose
        target the M1 bars since it opened reached before its stop blocks its side for ``tp_block_minutes``
        from that bar's close (C9). The same path runs in a replay and live (the live engine reads no deal
        history). ``m1``: bar-open index, high / low on bid - a sell's target counted on the bid low, the
        raise DataQualityError(f"no {entry_timeframe} bar of {symbol} closes at {decision_time.isoformat()}")
    states: list[TimeframeState] = []
    cut: dict[Timeframe, pd.DataFrame] = {}
    flags: list[str] = []
    for tf, frame in frames.items():
        n = frame.count_upto(decision_time)
        if n == 0:
            raise InsufficientDataError(f"no closed {tf} bar of {symbol} at {decision_time.isoformat()}")
        cut[tf] = frame.df.iloc[0 if window is None else max(0, n - window) : n].copy()
        bad = int((~cut[tf]["valid"].astype(bool)).sum())
        if bad:
            # indicators computed through a bad print stay contaminated: hold while it is in the window
            flags.append(f"{tf.value}:INVALID_OHLC:{bad}")
        states.append(_state(frame, n - 1))
        flags.extend(f"{tf.value}:{flag}" for flag in frame.quality_flags)
        lag = (decision_time - states[-1].bar_close_utc).total_seconds()
        if lag >= 2 * tf.seconds:
            flags.append(f"{tf.value}:ALIGNMENT_LAG:{int(lag // tf.seconds)}")
    first = 0 if window is None else max(0, n_entry - window)
    support, resistance = _levels(entry, n_entry - 1, params.sr_tolerance_atr, first)
    spread = None if quote is None or not quote.valid else quote.spread_points
    market = MarketContext(
        symbol=symbol,
        decision_time_utc=decision_time,
        entry_timeframe=entry_timeframe,
        higher_timeframe=higher_timeframe,
        states=tuple(states),
        session=trading_session(decision_time),
        bid=None if quote is None or not quote.valid else quote.bid,
        ask=None if quote is None or not quote.valid else quote.ask,
        spread_points=spread,
        quote_time_utc=None if quote is None else quote.time_utc,
        quality_flags=tuple(flags),
        support_levels=support,
        resistance_levels=resistance,
        htf=htf,
    )
    return StrategyContext(market, cut, now_utc, spec, evidence or {})


class ContextBuilder:
    """Fetches, analyzes and aligns every enabled timeframe of a symbol (live engine and scanner)."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        candles: CandleSource,
        config: AppConfig,
        clock: Clock,
        quotes: QuoteSource | None = None,
        evidence: EvidenceEngine | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.candles = candles
        self.config = config
        self.clock = clock
        self.quotes = quotes
        self.evidence = evidence
        self.maps = MapBook.from_config(config)

    def analyze(self, symbol: str) -> dict[Timeframe, AnalyzedFrame]:
        tfs = self.config.timeframes
        out: dict[Timeframe, AnalyzedFrame] = {}
        for tf in tfs.enabled:
            frame = self.candles.closed_candles(symbol, tf, tfs.warmup_bars)
            if frame.df.empty:
                raise InsufficientDataError(f"no closed {tf} bars for {symbol}")
            out[tf] = analyze_frame(
                frame.df, tf, self.config.indicators, self.config.regime, frame.quality.flags
            )
        return out

    def build(self, symbol: str, spec: SymbolSpec | None = None) -> StrategyContext:
        frames = self.analyze(symbol)
        entry_tf = self.config.timeframes.entry
        decision_time = pd.Timestamp(frames[entry_tf].df.index[-1]).to_pydatetime()
        quote = self.quotes.quote(spec) if self.quotes is not None and spec is not None else None
        snapshots = self.evaluate_evidence(symbol, frames, decision_time)
        htf = self.market_map(symbol, decision_time, None if spec is None else spec.point)
        ctx = context_at(
            frames,
            symbol=symbol,
            entry_timeframe=entry_tf,
            higher_timeframe=self.config.timeframes.higher,
            decision_time=decision_time,
            now_utc=self.clock.now_utc(),
            params=self.config.indicators,
            quote=quote,
            spec=spec,
            evidence=snapshots,
            htf=htf,
        )
        log_analysis(ctx.market)
        return ctx

    def market_map(
        self, symbol: str, decision_time: datetime, point: float | None = None
                adj = better_stop(pos.side, adj, plan)
            if adj.close is not None:
                self.paper.request_close(pos.ticket, adj.close)
                continue
            if adj.new_sl is None:
                continue
            if (adj.new_sl - pos.sl) * pos.side.sign <= 0:
                log.error(
                    "refused an unfavourable stop move on #%s (%s -> %s)", pos.ticket, pos.sl, adj.new_sl
                )
                continue
            min_gap = (spec.stops_level + spec.freeze_level) * spec.point
            if (mark - adj.new_sl) * pos.side.sign <= min_gap:
                continue  # too close to the price for the broker to accept; retry on a later quote
            last = self._last_modify.get(pos.ticket)
            if last is not None and (now - last).total_seconds() < self.config.modify_min_interval_seconds:
                continue
            old = pos.sl
            self.paper.modify_stop(pos.ticket, adj.new_sl, adj.stop_kind)
            self._last_modify[pos.ticket] = now
            if self.bus is not None:
                self.bus.emit(
                    EventType.STOP_MOVED,
                    symbol=symbol,
                    ticket=pos.ticket,
                    old=old,
                    new=adj.new_sl,
                    kind=None if adj.stop_kind is None else adj.stop_kind.value,
                    note=adj.note,
                    paper=True,
                )

    def on_bar(self, symbol: str, ctx: StrategyContext) -> None:
        """A new closed entry bar: count it for the time stop and ask the owning strategy about closing."""
        for pos in [p for p in self.paper.broker.positions.values() if p.symbol == symbol]:
            pos.bars_held += 1
            strategy = self.strategies_by_magic.get(pos.request.magic)
            if strategy is not None and not pos.close_requested and strategy.should_close(ctx, pos.side):
                log.info("strategy %s closes #%s on %s", strategy.name, pos.ticket, symbol)
                self.paper.request_close(pos.ticket, ExitReason.SIGNAL)

    def on_lane_bar(self, symbol: str, ctx: StrategyContext, strategies: Collection[str]) -> None:
        """A lane's closed entry bar: the own management (``manage_position``) of that lane's strategies."""
        spec = self.specs[symbol]
        for pos in [p for p in self.paper.broker.positions.values() if p.symbol == symbol]:
            strategy = self.strategies_by_magic.get(pos.request.magic)
            if strategy is None or strategy.name not in strategies or pos.close_requested or pos.sl is None:
                continue
            mark = ctx.market.bid if pos.side is Side.BUY else ctx.market.ask
            if mark is None or pos.request.sl is None:
                continue
            view = OpenPosition(
                pos.side,
                pos.entry_price,
                pos.request.sl,
                pos.sl,
                pos.request.tp,
                pos.entry_time,
                pos.request.signal_id,
            )
            return
        self._last_modify[pos.ticket] = now
        result = self.orders.execution.send(
            self.orders.builder.modify_stops(spec, pos, new_sl, pos.tp if pos.tp > 0 else None)
        )
        if result.ok or result.retcode == 10025:
            if self.bus is not None:
                self.bus.emit(
                    EventType.STOP_MOVED,
                    symbol=pos.symbol,
                    ticket=pos.ticket,
                    old=pos.sl,
                    new=new_sl,
                    note=note,
                    paper=False,
                )
        else:
            log.warning("stop change on #%s refused: %s", pos.ticket, result.description)
            self.orders.monitor.observe_order_failure(f"modify #{pos.ticket}: {result.description}")

    # --- every bar --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def on_bar(self, symbol: str, ctx: StrategyContext) -> None:
        spec = self.specs.get(symbol)
        if spec is None:
            return
        for pos in self.bot_positions(symbol):
            strategy = self.strategies_by_magic.get(pos.magic)
            if strategy is not None and strategy.should_close(ctx, pos.side):
                self.close(pos, spec, ExitReason.SIGNAL)

    def on_lane_bar(self, symbol: str, ctx: StrategyContext, strategies: Collection[str]) -> None:
        """A lane's closed entry bar: the own management of its strategies. The position's signal is not
        known here, so the strategy reads its plan from the position (``OpenPosition.signal_id`` empty)."""
        spec = self.specs.get(symbol)
        if spec is None:
            return
        initial = self.initial_stops()
        for pos in self.bot_positions(symbol):
            strategy = self.strategies_by_magic.get(pos.magic)
            first_sl = initial.get(pos.ticket)
            if strategy is None or strategy.name not in strategies or first_sl is None or pos.sl <= 0:
                continue
            mark = ctx.market.bid if pos.side is Side.BUY else ctx.market.ask
            if mark is None:
                continue
            tp = pos.tp if pos.tp > 0 else None
            view = OpenPosition(pos.side, pos.price_open, first_sl, pos.sl, tp, pos.time_utc)
            adj = from_advice(strategy.manage_position(ctx, view), pos.side, mark=mark, sl=pos.sl)
            if adj is None:
"""Strategy registry: builds the enabled strategies from ``config.yaml`` and runs them safely (PLAN Â§A7).

- The catalog is the set of strategy classes the code ships. ``strategies.items`` selects and parameterizes
  them; an unknown name, a duplicate entry, invalid params, a timeframe that is not enabled or a warm-up
  longer than ``timeframes.warmup_bars`` is a :class:`ConfigError` at startup.
- :meth:`StrategySet.evaluate` attaches the context's evidence to every signal (TAA-307). It is also the
  plugin boundary: an exception inside a strategy, or a signal that does not belong to the context it was
  given, becomes HOLD (``STRATEGY_ERROR``) and is logged with its traceback. One faulty strategy never
  stops the others or the engine loop.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from collections.abc import Iterable, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from pydantic import ValidationError

from app.config import ConfluenceConfig, StrategiesConfig, TimeframesConfig
from app.core.errors import ConfigError
from app.strategy.base_strategy import BaseStrategy
from app.strategy.enrichment import enrich
from app.strategy.signal_models import ReasonCode, Signal, StrategyContext

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class StrategyRegistry:
    def __init__(self, catalog: Iterable[type[BaseStrategy]]) -> None:
        self._classes: dict[str, type[BaseStrategy]] = {}
        for cls in catalog:
            if cls.name in self._classes:
                raise ConfigError(f"duplicate strategy name {cls.name!r}")
            self._classes[cls.name] = cls

    @property
    def names(self) -> list[str]:
        return sorted(self._classes)

    def get(self, name: str) -> type[BaseStrategy]:
        try:
            return self._classes[name]
        except KeyError:
            raise ConfigError(f"unknown strategy {name!r}; available: {self.names}") from None

    def create(self, name: str, raw_params: dict[str, object], expiry_bars: int) -> BaseStrategy:
        cls = self.get(name)
        try:
            params = cls.Params.model_validate({"expiry_bars": expiry_bars, **raw_params})
        except ValidationError as exc:
            raise ConfigError(f"invalid params for strategy {name}: {exc}") from exc
        return cls(params)

    def from_config(
        self,
        strategies: StrategiesConfig,
        timeframes: TimeframesConfig,
        confluence: ConfluenceConfig | None = None,
    ) -> StrategySet:
        seen: set[str] = set()
        built: list[BaseStrategy] = []
        for item in strategies.items:
            if item.name in seen:
                raise ConfigError(f"strategy {item.name!r} is listed twice")
            seen.add(item.name)
            strategy = self.create(item.name, item.params, strategies.signal_expiry_bars)
            missing = [tf for tf in strategy.required_timeframes() if tf not in timeframes.enabled]
            if missing:
                raise ConfigError(f"strategy {item.name} needs timeframes {missing} that are not enabled")
            if strategy.warmup_bars() > timeframes.warmup_bars:
                raise ConfigError(
                    f"strategy {item.name} needs {strategy.warmup_bars()} warm-up bars; "
                    f"timeframes.warmup_bars is {timeframes.warmup_bars}"
                )
            if item.enabled:
                built.append(strategy)
        return StrategySet(tuple(built), confluence or ConfluenceConfig())


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class StrategySet:
    strategies: Sequence[BaseStrategy]
    confluence: ConfluenceConfig = field(default_factory=ConfluenceConfig)

    @property
    def names(self) -> list[str]:
        return [s.name for s in self.strategies]

    def required_detectors(self) -> frozenset[str]:
        return frozenset().union(*(s.required_detectors() for s in self.strategies))

    def evaluate(self, ctx: StrategyContext) -> list[Signal]:
        """One signal per strategy, enriched with the context's evidence (confluence setup strength)."""
        return [
            enrich(self._evaluate_one(s, ctx), ctx, self.confluence, s.core_families) for s in self.strategies
        ]

    @staticmethod
    def _evaluate_one(strategy: BaseStrategy, ctx: StrategyContext) -> Signal:
