class SymbolsConfig(StrictModel):
    allowed: list[str] = Field(default_factory=lambda: ["EURUSD", "GBPUSD", "USDJPY", "XAUUSD"], min_length=1)
    reference_symbol: str = Field(default="EURUSD", description="liquid symbol used to verify server time")
    clock_fallback_symbols: list[str] = Field(
        default_factory=lambda: ["BTCUSD", "ETHUSD"],
        max_length=5,
        description="24/7 symbols that verify server time while the reference symbol's market is closed",
    )
    overrides: dict[str, SymbolOverride] = Field(default_factory=dict)

    @field_validator("allowed", "clock_fallback_symbols")
    @classmethod
    def _normalize(cls, value: list[str]) -> list[str]:
        cleaned = [s.strip() for s in value if s.strip()]
        if len(set(cleaned)) != len(cleaned):
            raise ValueError("duplicate symbols in list")
        return cleaned

    @property
    def clock_symbols(self) -> list[str]:
        """Symbols to verify server time with, in order of preference."""
        return [
            self.reference_symbol,
            *(s for s in self.clock_fallback_symbols if s != self.reference_symbol),
        ]


class TimeframesConfig(StrictModel):
    higher: Timeframe = Timeframe.H1
    entry: Timeframe = Timeframe.M15
    refinement: Timeframe | None = None
    warmup_bars: int = Field(default=400, ge=50, le=20_000)
    candle_close_grace_seconds: float = Field(default=3.0, ge=0, le=60)
    stale_tick_seconds: float = Field(default=120.0, gt=0)
    max_gap_bars: int = Field(default=3, ge=0)  # the longest hole in trading hours (quality.py)
    # TAA-L809: higher-timeframe market map (trend vs consolidation, demand/supply zones) for every setup.
    # Measured on every signal (ctx: features); never a strategy frame, so it adds no quality flags.
    context: list[Timeframe] = Field(default_factory=list, description="empty: no market map (today)")
    context_bars: int = Field(default=700, ge=300, le=5000, description="closed bars per context timeframe")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _order(self) -> TimeframesConfig:
        if self.higher.seconds <= self.entry.seconds:
            raise ValueError("higher timeframe must be longer than the entry timeframe")
        if self.refinement and self.refinement.seconds >= self.entry.seconds:
            raise ValueError("refinement timeframe must be shorter than the entry timeframe")
        if len(set(self.context)) != len(self.context):
            raise ValueError("timeframes.context: no duplicates")
        for tf in self.context:
            if tf.seconds <= self.entry.seconds:
                raise ValueError(f"timeframes.context: {tf} must be longer than the entry timeframe")
        return self

    @property
    def enabled(self) -> list[Timeframe]:
        tfs = [self.higher, self.entry]
        if self.refinement:
            tfs.append(self.refinement)
        return tfs


class IndicatorParams(StrictModel):
    ema_fast: int = Field(default=20, ge=2)
    ema_mid: int = Field(default=50, ge=2)
    ema_slow: int = Field(default=200, ge=2)
    rsi_period: int = Field(default=14, ge=2)
    atr_period: int = Field(default=14, ge=2)
    adx_period: int = Field(default=14, ge=2)
    macd_fast: int = Field(default=12, ge=2)
    macd_slow: int = Field(default=26, ge=3)
    macd_signal: int = Field(default=9, ge=2)
    stoch_k: int = Field(default=14, ge=2)
    stoch_k_smooth: int = Field(default=3, ge=1)
    stoch_d: int = Field(default=3, ge=1)
) -> AnalyzedFrame:
    """Indicators, states and swings for one timeframe of closed candles (``CANDLE_COLUMNS`` input)."""
    if "close_time" not in candles.columns:
        raise DataQualityError("candles need a close_time column")
    index = pd.DatetimeIndex(candles["close_time"])
    if index.tz is None:
        raise DataQualityError("close_time must be timezone-aware UTC")
    index = index.tz_convert("UTC")
    if not (index.is_monotonic_increasing and index.is_unique):
        raise DataQualityError(f"{tf} candles are not in strictly increasing close-time order")
    src = candles.reset_index(drop=True)
    df = pd.DataFrame(index=index)
    df["open_time"] = (
        pd.DatetimeIndex(src["open_time"]).tz_convert("UTC")
        if "open_time" in src
        else index - pd.Timedelta(seconds=tf.seconds)
    )
    for col in ("open", "high", "low", "close"):
        df[col] = src[col].to_numpy(dtype=float)
    for col in ("tick_volume", "spread"):
        df[col] = src[col].to_numpy(dtype=float) if col in src else np.nan
    high, low, close = df["high"], df["low"], df["close"]
    df["ema_fast"] = ema(close, params.ema_fast)
    df["ema_mid"] = ema(close, params.ema_mid)
    df["ema_slow"] = ema(close, params.ema_slow)
    df["rsi"] = rsi(close, params.rsi_period)
    df["atr"] = atr(high, low, close, params.atr_period)
    df["atr_pct"] = atr_percentile(df["atr"], params.atr_percentile_lookback)
    dmi = adx(high, low, close, params.adx_period)
    df["adx"], df["plus_di"], df["minus_di"] = dmi["adx"], dmi["plus_di"], dmi["minus_di"]
    df["structure"] = market_structure(high, low, params.swing_k)["trend"]
    c, mid, slow = close.to_numpy(), df["ema_mid"].to_numpy(), df["ema_slow"].to_numpy()
    df["trend"] = trend_series(c, mid, slow)
    df["regime"] = regime_series(df["adx"].to_numpy(), df["atr_pct"].to_numpy(), regime)
    df["volatility"] = volatility_series(df["atr_pct"].to_numpy(), regime)
    o, h, lo = df["open"].to_numpy(), high.to_numpy(), low.to_numpy()
    finite = np.isfinite(o) & np.isfinite(h) & np.isfinite(lo) & np.isfinite(c)
    with np.errstate(invalid="ignore"):
        consistent = (lo > 0) & (lo <= np.minimum(o, c)) & (h >= np.maximum(o, c))
    df["valid"] = finite & consistent
    swings = tuple(find_swings(high, low, params.swing_k))
    return AnalyzedFrame(tf, df, swings, tuple(quality_flags))


def trading_session(at_utc: datetime) -> Session:
    """Main FX session at *at_utc*, from each centre's local business hours (zoneinfo handles DST)."""
    at = ensure_utc(at_utc)
    active = {s for s, tz, start, end in _SESSION_HOURS if _within(at, tz, start, end)}
    if {Session.LONDON, Session.NEW_YORK} <= active:
        return Session.LONDON_NY_OVERLAP
    for session in (Session.LONDON, Session.NEW_YORK, Session.ASIA):
        if session in active:
            return session
    return Session.OFF


_SESSION_HOURS = (
    (Session.ASIA, "Asia/Tokyo", time(9), time(18)),
    (Session.LONDON, "Europe/London", time(8), time(17)),
    (Session.NEW_YORK, "America/New_York", time(8), time(17)),
)


def _within(at: datetime, tz: str, start: time, end: time) -> bool:
    local = at.astimezone(ZoneInfo(tz))
    return local.weekday() < 5 and start <= local.time() < end


def _opt(value: float | None) -> float | None:
    if value is None:
        return None
    f = float(value)
    return f if math.isfinite(f) else None


def _state(frame: AnalyzedFrame, pos: int) -> TimeframeState:
    row = frame.df.iloc[pos]
    structure = row["structure"]
    return TimeframeState(
        timeframe=frame.timeframe,
        bar_close_utc=pd.Timestamp(frame.df.index[pos]).to_pydatetime(),
        close=float(row["close"]),
        trend=Trend(row["trend"]),
        regime=Regime(row["regime"]),
        volatility=VolatilityState(row["volatility"]),
        structure=None if pd.isna(structure) else str(structure),
        atr=_opt(row["atr"]),
        atr_percentile=_opt(row["atr_pct"]),
        adx=_opt(row["adx"]),
        plus_di=_opt(row["plus_di"]),
        minus_di=_opt(row["minus_di"]),
        rsi=_opt(row["rsi"]),
        ema_fast=_opt(row["ema_fast"]),
        ema_mid=_opt(row["ema_mid"]),
        ema_slow=_opt(row["ema_slow"]),
            previous_mid=None,
            atr=atr,
            median_spread=self.quotes.median_spread(symbol),
        )
        if quote.valid:
            self._last_quotes[symbol] = quote
            self.backend.on_quote(symbol, quote.bid, quote.ask, atr, now)
            self._quote_entries(symbol, quote.time_utc, quote.bid, quote.ask)

    @staticmethod
    def _quote_signal(
        strategy: OwnS2 | AITrader, ctx: StrategyContext, entry: StraddleEntry | AIEntry
    ) -> Signal | None:
        """The quote strategy's own signal for its own entry (each returns only its own kind)."""
        if isinstance(strategy, AITrader):
            return strategy.entry_signal(ctx, entry) if isinstance(entry, AIEntry) else None
        return strategy.entry_signal(ctx, entry) if isinstance(entry, StraddleEntry) else None

    def _quote_entries(self, symbol: str, at: datetime, bid: float, ask: float) -> None:
        """Feed the quote to the quote-driven strategies; an entry they report goes the bar path's way."""
        disabled = self.disabled_strategies()
        for lane, strategy in self._quote_lanes:
            if not lane.trades(symbol) or strategy.name in disabled:
                continue
            entry = strategy.on_quote(symbol, at, bid, ask)
            if entry is None:
                continue
            try:
                ctx = lane.builder.build(symbol, self.symbols[symbol])
            except TaaError as exc:
                log.warning(
                    "no context for %s's %s entry on %s: %s", strategy.name, entry.side.value, symbol, exc
                )
                continue
            signal = self._quote_signal(strategy, ctx, entry)
            if signal is not None:
                self._decide(symbol, lane, ctx, signal)

    def _stream_candles(self) -> None:
        if self.candle_stream is None:
            return
        try:
            self.candle_stream.tick(self.symbols)
        except Exception:  # sync boundary: charts may lag, trading goes on
            log.exception("candle stream failed")
            self.candle_stream.failures += 1

    @property
    def lanes(self) -> list[EntryLane]:
        """The main entry timeframe (the engine's own strategies, arbiter, context), then the extra lanes."""
        return [
            EntryLane(MAIN, self.config, self.strategies, self.arbiter, self.builder, None),
            *self.extra_lanes,
        ]

    @property
    def strategy_names(self) -> list[str]:
        """Every strategy the engine runs: the main entry timeframe's, then each lane's."""
        return [n for lane in self.lanes for n in lane.strategies.names]

    def _check_new_bar(self, symbol: str) -> None:
        for lane in self.lanes:
            if not lane.trades(symbol):
                continue
            tf = lane.entry
            frame = self.candles.closed_candles(symbol, tf, 2)
            opened = frame.last_open_time
            if opened is None or not self.watermarks.is_new(symbol, tf, opened):
                continue
            try:
                self._evaluate(symbol, lane)
            finally:
                # a bar is evaluated at most once, even when the evaluation failed (fail closed: no retry
                # storm)
                self.watermarks.mark(symbol, tf, opened)

    def _evaluate(self, symbol: str, lane: EntryLane | None = None) -> None:
        lane = lane or self.lanes[0]
        spec = self.symbols[symbol]
        try:
            ctx = lane.builder.build(symbol, spec)
        except TaaError as exc:
            log.warning("no context for %s (%s lane): %s", symbol, lane.name, exc)
            return
        if lane.name == MAIN:  # the backend's bar (paper fills, bars held) and the quote ATR: main timeframe
            if ctx.market.atr is not None:
                self._atr[symbol] = ctx.market.atr
            self.backend.on_bar(symbol, ctx)
        self.backend.on_lane_bar(symbol, ctx, lane.strategies.names)  # each strategy's own management
        self._attach_entries(symbol, lane)
        disabled = self.disabled_strategies()
        signals = [  # a remotely disabled strategy opens nothing; its close signals still count
            s for s in lane.strategies.evaluate(ctx) if not (s.is_entry and s.strategy in disabled)
        ]
        selected = lane.arbiter.arbitrate(signals).selected
        self.on_arbitrated(lane)
        if selected is not None:
            self._decide(symbol, lane, ctx, selected)

    def _attach_entries(self, symbol: str, lane: EntryLane) -> None:
        """Strategies with an ``entry_group`` read the group's accepted entries of the last day from the
        decision store (not from memory: the rule holds across a restart)."""
        since = self.clock.now_utc() - ENTRY_LOOKBACK
        for strategy in lane.strategies.strategies:
            if strategy.entry_group:
                strategy.attach_entries(
                    symbol, self.decision_store.accepted_entries(strategy.entry_group, symbol, since)
                )

    def _decide(self, symbol: str, lane: EntryLane, ctx: StrategyContext, selected: Signal) -> None:
        """The decision engine on one selected signal, and the order when it is accepted."""
        spec = self.symbols[symbol]
        applied = None if self.risk_limits is None else self.risk_limits.current()
        record = self.decisions.decide(
            DecisionRequest(
                signal=selected,
                market=ctx.market,
                spec=spec,
                quote=self.quotes.quote(spec),
                account=AccountState(
                    self.backend.funds(),
                    self._book(),
                    self._loss_status(),
                    self._pending_orders(),
                    self.backend.position_groups(),
                ),
                health=self.health_snapshot(),
                specs=self.symbols,
                gate=self.gate(),
                last_entry_at=self._last_entry.get(symbol),
                profile_limits=None if applied is None else applied.limits,
                risk_source=None if applied is None else applied.source,
                cage=None if applied is None else applied.cage,
                probation=self._probation(),
            )
        )
        if record.decision is Decision.ACCEPT:
            magic = self.magic[selected.strategy]
            if self.backend.place(record, spec, magic):
                self._last_entry[symbol] = ctx.decision_time_utc
                self.on_entry(symbol, ctx.decision_time_utc)
                self.bus.emit(
                    EventType.SIGNAL_ACCEPTED,
                    symbol=symbol,
                    strategy=selected.strategy,
                    side=selected.action.value,
                    volume=str(record.volume),
                    decision_id=record.decision_id,
                    paper=self.settings.mode is TradingMode.PAPER,
                )

    def on_arbitrated(self, lane: EntryLane | None = None) -> None:
        lane = lane or self.lanes[0]
        self.state.save(lane.state_key, lane.arbiter.cooldown_state())

    def on_entry(self, symbol: str, at: datetime) -> None:
        self.state.save("last_entry", {sym: t.isoformat() for sym, t in self._last_entry.items()})

    def _scan_limits(self) -> ProfileLimits | None:
        """The owner's limits for the scanner's ADVISORY decisions: the owner's sizing and account limits, but
        not min RR, a HARD check there that would hide opportunities other users' profiles accept, and not
        the entry plan (alerts carry each user's own plan, TAA-1207); always STANDARD (PLAN Â§A36)."""
        if self.risk_limits is None:
            return None
        return dataclasses.replace(self.risk_limits.scan_limits(), min_risk_reward=None, entry_plan=None)

    def effective_risk(self) -> RiskConfig:
        """The limits the engine trades with now (TAA-710); ``config.yaml`` alone before ``start()``."""
        return self.config.risk if self.risk_limits is None else self.risk_limits.current().effective

    def _pending_orders(self) -> list[BrokerOrder]:
        try:
            return self.backend.pending_orders()
        except TaaError:
            log.warning("could not read resting orders for exposure")
            return []

    def _book(self) -> list[BrokerPosition]:
        try:
            return self.backend.book()
    # basis (None: margin alone decides, the standard cage)
    min_lot_max_risk_percent: float | None = Field(default=None, gt=0, le=100)
    require_take_profit: bool = True
    sizing_basis: Literal["min_equity_balance", "equity", "balance"] = "min_equity_balance"
    tick_value_tolerance: float = Field(default=0.10, gt=0, le=0.5)
    max_sl_atr_multiple: float = Field(default=3.0, gt=0)
    price_drift_atr: float = Field(default=0.5, gt=0)
    correlation_groups: dict[str, list[str]] = Field(
        default_factory=lambda: {"EUR_GBP": ["EURUSD", "GBPUSD"]}
    )
    max_same_direction_per_currency: int = Field(default=2, ge=1)
    probation_trades: int = Field(default=20, ge=0)
    probation_multiplier: float = Field(default=0.25, gt=0, le=1)
    foreign_positions_policy: Literal["count", "halt"] = "count"
    # None: fades meet the normal min RR and SL cap, i.e. they cannot trade (code default)
    fade: FadeRiskConfig | None = None

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _coherence(self) -> RiskConfig:
        if self.max_risk_per_trade_percent > self.max_daily_loss_percent:
            raise ValueError("max_risk_per_trade_percent must not exceed max_daily_loss_percent")
        if self.max_daily_loss_percent > self.max_weekly_loss_percent:
            raise ValueError("max_daily_loss_percent must not exceed max_weekly_loss_percent")
        if self.max_total_open_risk_percent < self.max_risk_per_trade_percent:
            raise ValueError("max_total_open_risk_percent must be >= max_risk_per_trade_percent")
        if self.min_lot is not None and self.min_lot > self.max_lot:
            raise ValueError("min_lot must not exceed max_lot")
        return self


class HighRiskCage(RiskConfig):
    """The outer cage of the aggressive Trading-profile preset (PLAN Â§A36). Built only by
    :meth:`HighRiskConfig.cage` from ``config.yaml`` â†’ ``high_risk`` plus the env opt-in; ``risk:`` always
    validates as a plain :class:`RiskConfig`, so no edit of that block can raise its ceilings to these.

    Inside it: the minimum lot when the budget buys less, but only while it risks at most
    ``min_lot_max_risk_percent`` of the account (a 50-100 USD account must still trade; owner, 2026-10-14),
    one trade per currency direction, a notional leverage cap, and the setups the preset may trade
    (``setups``). Fades risk ``fade_risk_factor`` x the preset's risk per trade.
    """

    max_risk_per_trade_percent: float = Field(default=10.0, gt=0, le=HIGH_RISK_CEILING_RISK_PER_TRADE_PCT)
    max_total_open_risk_percent: float = Field(default=20.0, gt=0, le=HIGH_RISK_CEILING_TOTAL_OPEN_RISK_PCT)
    max_open_positions: int = Field(default=3, ge=1, le=HIGH_RISK_MAX_OPEN_POSITIONS)
    max_daily_loss_percent: float = Field(default=20.0, gt=0, le=HIGH_RISK_CEILING_DAILY_LOSS_PCT)
    max_weekly_loss_percent: float = Field(default=35.0, gt=0, le=HIGH_RISK_CEILING_WEEKLY_LOSS_PCT)
    max_account_drawdown_percent: float = Field(default=60.0, gt=0, le=HIGH_RISK_CEILING_DRAWDOWN_PCT)
    max_effective_leverage: float | None = Field(
        default=HIGH_RISK_MAX_LEVERAGE, gt=0, le=HIGH_RISK_MAX_LEVERAGE
    )
    min_lot_fallback: bool = True
    min_lot_max_risk_percent: float | None = Field(
        default=HIGH_RISK_DEFAULT_MIN_LOT_RISK_PCT, gt=0, le=HIGH_RISK_CEILING_MIN_LOT_RISK_PCT
    )
    max_same_direction_per_currency: int = Field(default=1, ge=1, le=1)
    setups: tuple[str, ...] = Field(default=(), min_length=1)
    fade_risk_factor: float = Field(default=0.5, gt=0, le=1)

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _cage_rules(self) -> HighRiskCage:
        if self.min_lot_fallback and self.min_lot_max_risk_percent is None:
            raise ValueError("the high-risk cage opens the minimum lot above the budget only with a risk cap")
        if self.max_effective_leverage is None:
            raise ValueError("the high-risk cage needs a leverage cap")
        return self


class HighRiskConfig(StrictModel):
    """``config.yaml`` â†’ ``high_risk`` (PLAN Â§A36): the aggressive preset's cage. Absent: the preset is not
    available on this machine. It applies only with ``ENABLE_HIGH_RISK_PROFILE`` and the account-bound
    ``HIGH_RISK_CONFIRMATION``, in one of ``modes`` (PAPER/DEMO only in v1: LIVE is refused)."""

    max_risk_per_trade_percent: float = Field(default=10.0, gt=0, le=HIGH_RISK_CEILING_RISK_PER_TRADE_PCT)
    max_total_open_risk_percent: float = Field(default=20.0, gt=0, le=HIGH_RISK_CEILING_TOTAL_OPEN_RISK_PCT)
    max_open_positions: int = Field(default=3, ge=1, le=HIGH_RISK_MAX_OPEN_POSITIONS)
    max_daily_loss_percent: float = Field(default=20.0, gt=0, le=HIGH_RISK_CEILING_DAILY_LOSS_PCT)
    max_weekly_loss_percent: float = Field(default=35.0, gt=0, le=HIGH_RISK_CEILING_WEEKLY_LOSS_PCT)
    max_account_drawdown_percent: float = Field(default=60.0, gt=0, le=HIGH_RISK_CEILING_DRAWDOWN_PCT)
    max_effective_leverage: float = Field(default=HIGH_RISK_MAX_LEVERAGE, gt=0, le=HIGH_RISK_MAX_LEVERAGE)
    min_lot_max_risk_percent: float = Field(
        default=HIGH_RISK_DEFAULT_MIN_LOT_RISK_PCT, gt=0, le=HIGH_RISK_CEILING_MIN_LOT_RISK_PCT
    )
    modes: list[TradingMode] = Field(
        default_factory=lambda: [TradingMode.PAPER, TradingMode.DEMO], min_length=1
    )
    setups: list[str] = Field(min_length=1, description="the proven setups the preset may trade")
    fade_risk_factor: float = Field(default=0.5, gt=0, le=1, description="fades risk this x the preset")
    # unset: the standard risk: values (500 % / 30 %); the sizer cuts the lot to whatever these allow
    min_margin_level_percent: float | None = Field(
        default=None, ge=HIGH_RISK_FLOOR_MARGIN_LEVEL_PCT, le=10_000, description="after the trade"
    )
    max_margin_utilization_percent: float | None = Field(
        default=None, gt=0, le=HIGH_RISK_CEILING_MARGIN_UTILIZATION_PCT, description="% of free margin"
    )

        refused = sorted(m.value for m in value if m not in (TradingMode.PAPER, TradingMode.DEMO))
        if refused:

    def on_bar(self, symbol: str, ctx: StrategyContext) -> None:
        """A new closed entry bar: count it for the time stop and ask the owning strategy about closing."""
        for pos in [p for p in self.paper.broker.positions.values() if p.symbol == symbol]:
            pos.bars_held += 1
            strategy = self.strategies_by_magic.get(pos.request.magic)
            if strategy is not None and not pos.close_requested and strategy.should_close(ctx, pos.side):
                log.info("strategy %s closes #%s on %s", strategy.name, pos.ticket, symbol)
                self.paper.request_close(pos.ticket, ExitReason.SIGNAL)

    def on_lane_bar(self, symbol: str, ctx: StrategyContext, strategies: Collection[str]) -> None:
        """A lane's closed entry bar: the own management (``manage_position``) of that lane's strategies."""
        spec = self.specs[symbol]
        for pos in [p for p in self.paper.broker.positions.values() if p.symbol == symbol]:
            strategy = self.strategies_by_magic.get(pos.request.magic)
            if strategy is None or strategy.name not in strategies or pos.close_requested or pos.sl is None:
                continue
            mark = ctx.market.bid if pos.side is Side.BUY else ctx.market.ask
            if mark is None or pos.request.sl is None:
                continue
            view = OpenPosition(
                pos.side,
                pos.entry_price,
                pos.request.sl,
                pos.sl,
                pos.request.tp,
                pos.entry_time,
                pos.request.signal_id,
            )
            adj = from_advice(strategy.manage_position(ctx, view), pos.side, mark=mark, sl=pos.sl)
            if adj is None:
                continue
            if adj.close is not None:
                log.info("strategy %s closes #%s: %s", strategy.name, pos.ticket, adj.note)
                self.paper.request_close(pos.ticket, adj.close)
            elif adj.new_sl is not None:
                if (mark - adj.new_sl) * pos.side.sign <= (spec.stops_level + spec.freeze_level) * spec.point:
                    self.paper.request_close(pos.ticket, adj.stop_kind or ExitReason.SIGNAL)
                    continue
                self.paper.modify_stop(pos.ticket, adj.new_sl, adj.stop_kind)
                self._last_modify[pos.ticket] = self.clock.now_utc()

    def _missing_stop(self, ticket: int, initial: float | None) -> None:
        if initial is not None:
            log.critical("position #%s had no stop; re-attached %s from its intent", ticket, initial)
            self.paper.modify_stop(ticket, initial, None)
        else:
            log.critical("position #%s has no stop and none to re-attach: closing it", ticket)
            self.paper.request_close(ticket, ExitReason.STOP_LOSS)
                    note=note,
                    paper=False,
                )
        else:
            log.warning("stop change on #%s refused: %s", pos.ticket, result.description)
            self.orders.monitor.observe_order_failure(f"modify #{pos.ticket}: {result.description}")

    # --- every bar --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def on_bar(self, symbol: str, ctx: StrategyContext) -> None:
        spec = self.specs.get(symbol)
        if spec is None:
            return
        for pos in self.bot_positions(symbol):
            strategy = self.strategies_by_magic.get(pos.magic)
            if strategy is not None and strategy.should_close(ctx, pos.side):
                self.close(pos, spec, ExitReason.SIGNAL)

    def on_lane_bar(self, symbol: str, ctx: StrategyContext, strategies: Collection[str]) -> None:
        """A lane's closed entry bar: the own management of its strategies. The position's signal is not
        known here, so the strategy reads its plan from the position (``OpenPosition.signal_id`` empty)."""
        spec = self.specs.get(symbol)
        if spec is None:
            return
        initial = self.initial_stops()
        for pos in self.bot_positions(symbol):
            strategy = self.strategies_by_magic.get(pos.magic)
            first_sl = initial.get(pos.ticket)
            if strategy is None or strategy.name not in strategies or first_sl is None or pos.sl <= 0:
                continue
            mark = ctx.market.bid if pos.side is Side.BUY else ctx.market.ask
            if mark is None:
                continue
            tp = pos.tp if pos.tp > 0 else None
            view = OpenPosition(pos.side, pos.price_open, first_sl, pos.sl, tp, pos.time_utc)
            adj = from_advice(strategy.manage_position(ctx, view), pos.side, mark=mark, sl=pos.sl)
            if adj is None:
                continue
            if adj.close is not None:
                self.close(pos, spec, adj.close)
            elif adj.new_sl is not None:
                self._modify(pos, spec, adj.new_sl, mark, adj.note)

    # --- closing ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def close(self, pos: BrokerPosition, spec: SymbolSpec, reason: ExitReason) -> SendResult:
        tick = self.orders.market.tick(pos.symbol)
        price = pos.price_current if tick is None else (tick.bid if pos.side is Side.BUY else tick.ask)
        result = self.orders.execution.send(
            self.orders.builder.close(spec, pos, price, comment=f"taa:{reason.value.lower()}")
        )
        if result.ok:
            if self.bus is not None:
                self.bus.emit(
                    EventType.POSITION_CLOSED,
                    symbol=pos.symbol,
                    ticket=pos.ticket,
                    reason=reason.value,
                    paper=False,
                )
        else:
            log.error("close of #%s (%s) failed: %s", pos.ticket, reason.value, result.description)
            self.orders.monitor.observe_order_failure(f"close #{pos.ticket}: {result.description}")
        return result

index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..f306e1204df375e99e63d55d711247f32fc74d3f

+++ b/research/2026-10-26-three-bots/review_plant_bugs.py

+"""Crash-safe mutation check for the research session's three newly covered behaviors.
+
+Run from the repo with ``.venv\\Scripts\\python research\\2026-10-26-three-bots\\review_plant_bugs.py``.
+Each mutation is restored byte-for-byte, including after an interrupted run on the next invocation.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import subprocess  # nosec B404 - runs this repo's own pytest
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+BACKUP = HERE / ".review_bugs_backup"
+TREND = "app/strategy/trend_box.py"
+RANGE = "app/strategy/range_break.py"
+TREND_TEST = "tests/unit/test_strategy_trend_box.py"
+RANGE_TEST = "tests/unit/test_strategy_range_break.py"
+
+BUGS: list[tuple[str, str, bytes, bytes, str, str]] = [
+    (
+        "remove the trend box * leg height cap",
+        TREND,
+        b"top - floor <= self.params.box * leg",
+        b"top - floor <= leg",
+        TREND_TEST,
+        "test_a_box_taller_than_the_cap_is_not_a_box",
+    ),
+    (
+        "invert the trend box height cap comparison",
+        TREND,
+        b"top - floor <= self.params.box * leg",
+        b"top - floor >= self.params.box * leg",
+        TREND_TEST,
+        "test_a_box_taller_than_the_cap_is_not_a_box",
+    ),
+    (
+        "resample H1 close times without shifting them to open times",
+        RANGE,
+        b"- pd.Timedelta(hours=1)",
+        b"- pd.Timedelta(hours=0)",
+        RANGE_TEST,
+        "test_h4_is_built_from_h1_open_times_and_only_from_closed_bars",
+    ),
+    (
+        "use first H1 high instead of the H4 maximum",
+        RANGE,
+        b'"high": "max"',
+        b'"high": "first"',
+        RANGE_TEST,
+        "test_h4_is_built_from_h1_open_times_and_only_from_closed_bars",
+    ),
+    (
+        "pass a forming H4 bar based on its open time",
+        RANGE,
+        b"(h4.index + pd.Timedelta(hours=4)) <= at",
+        b"h4.index <= at",
+        RANGE_TEST,
+        "test_an_unclosed_h4_bar_never_reaches_the_zigzag",
+    ),
+    (
+        "ignore the configured range take-profit floor",
+        RANGE,
+        b"reward < self.params.min_take_profit",
+        b"False",
+        RANGE_TEST,
+        "test_sell_break_plan_is_the_mirror_and_reward_floor_is_enforced",
+    ),
+]
+
+
+def pytest(test: str, case: str | None = None) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]:
+    target = test if case is None else f"{test}::{case}"
+    return subprocess.run(
+        [sys.executable, "-m", "pytest", "-q", "-p", "no:cacheprovider", target],
+        cwd=ROOT,
+        capture_output=True,
+        text=True,
+        check=False,
+    )
+
+
+def restore_any() -> None:
+    if not BACKUP.exists():
+        return
+    for parked in sorted(BACKUP.glob("*.bak")):
+        relpath = parked.with_suffix("").name.replace("__", "/")
+        (ROOT / relpath).write_bytes(parked.read_bytes())
+        parked.unlink()
+        print(f"restored {relpath} from an interrupted run")
+
+
+def main() -> int:
+    sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
+    restore_any()
+    baseline = pytest(TREND_TEST) if False else subprocess.run(
+        [sys.executable, "-m", "pytest", "-q", "-p", "no:cacheprovider", TREND_TEST, RANGE_TEST],
+        cwd=ROOT,
+        capture_output=True,
+        text=True,
+        check=False,
+    )
+    if baseline.returncode:
+        raise RuntimeError(f"baseline strategy tests failed:\n{baseline.stdout}\n{baseline.stderr}")
+
+    BACKUP.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+    results: list[str] = []
+    missed = False
+    for name, relpath, old, new, test, case in BUGS:
+        target = ROOT / relpath
+        clean = target.read_bytes()
+        if clean.count(old) != 1 or old == new:
+            raise RuntimeError(f"mutation anchor is not unique for: {name}")
+        parked = BACKUP / (relpath.replace("/", "__") + ".bak")
+        if not parked.exists():
+            parked.write_bytes(clean)
+        try:
+            target.write_bytes(clean.replace(old, new, 1))
+            caught = pytest(test, case).returncode != 0
+            result = f"{'CAUGHT' if caught else 'MISSED'} | {name}"
+            results.append(result)
+            missed |= not caught
+        finally:
+            target.write_bytes(clean)
+            parked.unlink(missing_ok=True)
+        print(results[-1])
+
+    summary = f"\n{len(BUGS) - sum(r.startswith('MISSED') for r in results)}/{len(BUGS)} caught"
+    output = "\n".join([*results, summary]) + "\n"
+    (HERE / "review_plant_bugs_output.txt").write_text(output, encoding="utf-8")
+    print(summary)
+    return 1 if missed else 0
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    raise SystemExit(main())

apply patch

+            target.write_bytes(clean.replace(old, new, 1))
+            caught = pytest(test, case).returncode != 0
+            result = f"{'CAUGHT' if caught else 'MISSED'} | {name}"
+            results.append(result)
+            missed |= not caught
+        finally:
+            target.write_bytes(clean)
+            parked.unlink(missing_ok=True)
+        print(results[-1])
+
+    summary = f"\n{len(BUGS) - sum(r.startswith('MISSED') for r in results)}/{len(BUGS)} caught"
+    output = "\n".join([*results, summary]) + "\n"
+    (HERE / "review_plant_bugs_output.txt").write_text(output, encoding="utf-8")
+    print(summary)
+    return 1 if missed else 0
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    raise SystemExit(main())

+# Review findings — 2026-10-27
+
+## 1. Is turning the take-profit floor off safe?
+
+**Clean for division-by-zero and target-collapse in accepted entries; the floor is now genuinely off.**
+
+- **What I checked:** `app/ai/plan.py` compares `abs(tp - entry) < MIN_TAKE_PROFIT`; `app/ai/trader.py` sets that constant to `0.0` and adds the same strict `< 0` C5 veto. Since absolute reward is nonnegative, neither comparison rejects a positive reward. A zero-distance plan is still rejected by the following directional check: `(entry - tp if sell else tp - entry) <= 0`. In `AITrader.recheck_plan_stops`, the zero floor leaves the independent `plan_min_rr * risk` gate. At the fill check, `max(p.min_take_profit, p.plan_min_rr * abs(order.stop - price))` becomes `max(0, 0.2 * risk)` with defaults; that is still a positive R gate when risk is positive.
+- **Arithmetic:** `reach = abs(o.target - price) >= 0`; with `target_trim=0.10` and `min_take_profit=0`, `min(0, reach)=0` and `max(reach * (1 - 0.10), 0)=0.9 * reach`. There is no division. For every `reach > 0`, the trimmed target stays 90% of the way from fill to the target and cannot land on the fill. `target_trim < 1` is enforced by the parameter model. If `reach=0`, the expression yields zero and the target would equal the fill, but `AIOrder.geometry_ok(price)` rejects an order whose target is not strictly on its winning side before `entry_signal` applies this trim.
+- **Strategy floors:** `TrendBox` and `RangeBreak` also use `reward < min_take_profit`, so a 0.0 default makes that part of the condition false for nonnegative reward. Their independent positive `min_r` checks remain; their targets are built beyond the entry, and TrendBox also checks target direction. No calculation divides by the floor.
+- **Cost:** Sub-$5 targets can now pass the C5 distance checks. The AI lane still trims 10%, the AI path still applies its R checks, and the two strategies still apply their positive R floors. The claim that the floor rejected 1 of 496 signals is recorded in the research files; this review checked the changed arithmetic and call sites, not that historical count.
+
+## 2. Are the three new tests honest?
+
+**Yes. Each new test reaches the production behavior it claims to cover, and targeted mutations made each fail.**
+
+- **Trend box cap:** `test_a_box_taller_than_the_cap_is_not_a_box` calls `TrendBoxDown.evaluate` with a 13 USD box against a 12 USD cap, then raises the configured cap and expects entry on the same bars. The test does not calculate or substitute the production filter. Removing the `box * leg` cap and inverting its comparison each failed this single test.
+- **H1 close-to-open resampling:** `test_h4_is_built_from_h1_open_times_and_only_from_closed_bars` calls the production `RangeBreak._h4_from_h1` and checks exact H4 timestamps and aggregated highs. Replacing the one-hour shift with zero or replacing H4 maximum high with the first high failed this single test.
+- **Forming H4 exclusion:** `test_an_unclosed_h4_bar_never_reaches_the_zigzag` calls `RangeBreak.evaluate`, captures the high array passed to the production `zigzag`, and asserts the forming bar's sentinel high is absent and that 300 bars remain. Filtering by H4 open time instead of H4 close time failed this single test.
+- **Cost:** No flaw found in these tests' coverage of their stated production lines. The direct resampling test deliberately tests the helper rather than duplicating its grouping logic; the forming-bar test exercises the filter through `evaluate`.
+
+## 3. Plant bugs against the new tests
+
+**All six planted bugs were caught (6/6).** I added `research/2026-10-26-three-bots/review_plant_bugs.py`, which saves byte-for-byte backups before each mutation, restores in a `finally` block, and recovers leftover backups at the next invocation. The run output is in `research/2026-10-26-three-bots/review_plant_bugs_output.txt`.
+
+| Mutation | Single test run | Result |
+|---|---|---|
+| Remove the trend box `box * leg` cap | `test_a_box_taller_than_the_cap_is_not_a_box` | CAUGHT |
+| Invert the trend box cap comparison | `test_a_box_taller_than_the_cap_is_not_a_box` | CAUGHT |
+| Remove the H1 close-to-open shift | `test_h4_is_built_from_h1_open_times_and_only_from_closed_bars` | CAUGHT |
+| Aggregate H4 high with `first` instead of `max` | `test_h4_is_built_from_h1_open_times_and_only_from_closed_bars` | CAUGHT |
+| Include an H4 bar using its open time | `test_an_unclosed_h4_bar_never_reaches_the_zigzag` | CAUGHT |
+| Ignore the configured range take-profit floor | `test_sell_break_plan_is_the_mirror_and_reward_floor_is_enforced` | CAUGHT |
+
+The mutation run first passed both strategy test files, then ran each listed single test with only its corresponding production mutation. Every mutation was restored. No planted bug remains.
+
+## 4. Anything else wrong?
+
+**The range warmup and timeframe declarations line up, but daily flattening is unreachable for these extra-lane strategies. The lane items are currently disabled.**
+
+- **Warmup and frames:** `RangeBreak.required_timeframes()` asks for M1, M5, and H1, and `warmup_bars()` returns 1,200. The `three_bots` lane enables those frames through `higher: H1`, `entry: M5`, and `refinement: M1`, has no extra context frames, and sets `warmup_bars: 1200`. `ContextBuilder.analyze` requests the configured warmup count for each enabled timeframe, so RangeBreak receives up to 1,200 closed H1 bars; it resamples those to 300 closed H4 bars. TrendBox only needs 400 H1 bars. The three configured strategy names match the strategy class names. All three `enabled` fields are `false`, so the lane currently builds no active strategies until enabled.
+- **Daily close is unreachable in this lane:** M5 close timestamps can land exactly on 07:00 Bangkok, so the time comparison itself is representable. However, `Engine._evaluate` calls `backend.on_bar` only for the main lane. For every lane it calls `backend.on_lane_bar`; both paper and broker `on_lane_bar` implementations call `manage_position` but do not call `should_close`. The `should_close` calls are in `on_bar`. Since `three_bots` is an extra lane, enabling these strategies still will not invoke either `TrendBox.should_close` or `RangeBreak.should_close` through normal lane evaluation.
+- **Separate risk config change:** The same visible `config.yaml` diff raises `max_account_drawdown_percent` from 10 to 50, raises `max_consecutive_losses` from 4 to 50, and sets `consecutive_loss_pause_hours` from 24 to 0. The comment calls these breakers deactivated, but the change materially allows a deeper account drawdown and a much longer losing streak before the streak limit, with no cooldown. I did not change these settings.
+- **Cost:** If the three bots are enabled as configured, their stated daily flatten at 07:00 will not close their lane-owned positions via `should_close`; trades may remain open until another exit mechanism closes them. The higher drawdown and loss-streak limits increase exposure if those config changes are active.
+
+## Verification
+
+- `.venv\Scripts\python -m pytest -q tests/unit/test_strategy_trend_box.py tests/unit/test_strategy_range_break.py` — **36 passed**.
+- `.venv\Scripts\python research\2026-10-26-three-bots\review_plant_bugs.py` — **6/6 mutations caught**; source files restored.

+"""Crash-safe mutation check for the research session's three newly covered behaviors.
+
+Run from the repo with ``.venv\\Scripts\\python research\\2026-10-26-three-bots\\review_plant_bugs.py``.
+Each mutation is restored byte-for-byte, including after an interrupted run on the next invocation.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import subprocess  # nosec B404 - runs this repo's own pytest
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+BACKUP = HERE / ".review_bugs_backup"
+TREND = "app/strategy/trend_box.py"
+RANGE = "app/strategy/range_break.py"
+TREND_TEST = "tests/unit/test_strategy_trend_box.py"
+RANGE_TEST = "tests/unit/test_strategy_range_break.py"
+
+BUGS: list[tuple[str, str, bytes, bytes, str, str]] = [
+    (
+        "remove the trend box * leg height cap",
+        TREND,
+        b"top - floor <= self.params.box * leg",
+        b"top - floor <= leg",
+        TREND_TEST,
+        "test_a_box_taller_than_the_cap_is_not_a_box",
+    ),
+    (
+        "invert the trend box height cap comparison",
+        TREND,
+        b"top - floor <= self.params.box * leg",
+        b"top - floor >= self.params.box * leg",
+        TREND_TEST,
+        "test_a_box_taller_than_the_cap_is_not_a_box",
+    ),
+    (
+        "resample H1 close times without shifting them to open times",
+        RANGE,
+        b"- pd.Timedelta(hours=1)",
+        b"- pd.Timedelta(hours=0)",
+        RANGE_TEST,
+        "test_h4_is_built_from_h1_open_times_and_only_from_closed_bars",
+    ),
+    (
+        "use first H1 high instead of the H4 maximum",
+        RANGE,
+        b'"high": "max"',
+        b'"high": "first"',
+        RANGE_TEST,
+        "test_h4_is_built_from_h1_open_times_and_only_from_closed_bars",
+    ),
+    (
+        "pass a forming H4 bar based on its open time",
+        RANGE,
+        b"(h4.index + pd.Timedelta(hours=4)) <= at",
+        b"h4.index <= at",
+        RANGE_TEST,
+        "test_an_unclosed_h4_bar_never_reaches_the_zigzag",
+    ),
+    (
+        "ignore the configured range take-profit floor",
+        RANGE,
+        b"reward < self.params.min_take_profit",
+        b"False",
+        RANGE_TEST,
+        "test_sell_break_plan_is_the_mirror_and_reward_floor_is_enforced",
+    ),
+]
+
+
+def pytest(test: str, case: str | None = None) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]:
+    target = test if case is None else f"{test}::{case}"
+    return subprocess.run(
+        [sys.executable, "-m", "pytest", "-q", "-p", "no:cacheprovider", target],
+        cwd=ROOT,
+        capture_output=True,
+        text=True,
+        check=False,
+    )
+
+
+def restore_any() -> None:
+    if not BACKUP.exists():
+        return
+    for parked in sorted(BACKUP.glob("*.bak")):
+        relpath = parked.with_suffix("").name.replace("__", "/")
+        (ROOT / relpath).write_bytes(parked.read_bytes())
+        parked.unlink()
+        print(f"restored {relpath} from an interrupted run")
+
+
+def main() -> int:
+    sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
+    restore_any()
+    baseline = subprocess.run(
+        [sys.executable, "-m", "pytest", "-q", "-p", "no:cacheprovider", TREND_TEST, RANGE_TEST],
+        cwd=ROOT,
+        capture_output=True,
+        text=True,
+        check=False,
+    )
+    if baseline.returncode:
+        raise RuntimeError(f"baseline strategy tests failed:\n{baseline.stdout}\n{baseline.stderr}")
+
+    BACKUP.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+    results: list[str] = []
+    missed = False
+    for name, relpath, old, new, test, case in BUGS:
+        target = ROOT / relpath
+        clean = target.read_bytes()
+        if clean.count(old) != 1 or old == new:
+            raise RuntimeError(f"mutation anchor is not unique for: {name}")
+        parked = BACKUP / (relpath.replace("/", "__") + ".bak")
+        if not parked.exists():
+            parked.write_bytes(clean)
+        try:
+            target.write_bytes(clean.replace(old, new, 1))
+            caught = pytest(test, case).returncode != 0
+            result = f"{'CAUGHT' if caught else 'MISSED'} | {name}"
+            results.append(result)
+            missed |= not caught
+        finally:
+            target.write_bytes(clean)
+            parked.unlink(missing_ok=True)
+        print(results[-1])
+
+    summary = f"\n{len(BUGS) - sum(r.startswith('MISSED') for r in results)}/{len(BUGS)} caught"
+    output = "\n".join([*results, summary]) + "\n"
+    (HERE / "review_plant_bugs_output.txt").write_text(output, encoding="utf-8")
+    print(summary)
+    return 1 if missed else 0
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    raise SystemExit(main())

+"""Crash-safe mutation check for the research session's three newly covered behaviors.
+
+Run from the repo with ``.venv\\Scripts\\python research\\2026-10-26-three-bots\\review_plant_bugs.py``.
+Each mutation is restored byte-for-byte, including after an interrupted run on the next invocation.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import subprocess  # nosec B404 - runs this repo's own pytest
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+BACKUP = HERE / ".review_bugs_backup"
+TREND = "app/strategy/trend_box.py"
+RANGE = "app/strategy/range_break.py"
+TREND_TEST = "tests/unit/test_strategy_trend_box.py"
+RANGE_TEST = "tests/unit/test_strategy_range_break.py"
+
+BUGS: list[tuple[str, str, bytes, bytes, str, str]] = [
+    (
+        "invert the trend box height cap comparison",
+        TREND,
+        b"top - floor <= self.params.box * leg",
+        b"top - floor >= self.params.box * leg",
+        TREND_TEST,
+        "test_a_box_taller_than_the_cap_is_not_a_box",
+    ),
+    (
+        "add box share to leg instead of multiplying the cap",
+        TREND,
+        b"self.params.box * leg",
+        b"self.params.box + leg",
+        TREND_TEST,
+        "test_a_box_taller_than_the_cap_is_not_a_box",
+    ),
+    (
+        "label H4 bins by their close instead of their open",
+        RANGE,
+        b'opens.resample("4h").agg',
+        b'opens.resample("4h", label="right", closed="right").agg',
+        RANGE_TEST,
+        "test_h4_is_built_from_h1_open_times_and_only_from_closed_bars",
+    ),
+    (
+        "use first H1 high instead of the H4 maximum",
+        RANGE,
+        b'"high": "max"',
+        b'"high": "first"',
+        RANGE_TEST,
+        "test_h4_is_built_from_h1_open_times_and_only_from_closed_bars",
+    ),
+    (
+        "discard the latest H4 bar that has already closed",
+        RANGE,
+        b"(h4.index + pd.Timedelta(hours=4)) <= at",
+        b"(h4.index + pd.Timedelta(hours=5)) <= at",
+        RANGE_TEST,
+        "test_an_unclosed_h4_bar_never_reaches_the_zigzag",
+    ),
+    (
+        "divide reward by the disabled take-profit floor",
+        RANGE,
+        b"reward < self.params.min_take_profit",
+        b"reward / self.params.min_take_profit < 1.0",
+        RANGE_TEST,
+        "test_sell_break_plan_is_the_mirror_and_reward_floor_is_enforced",
+    ),
+]
+
+
+def pytest(test: str, case: str | None = None) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]:
+    target = test if case is None else f"{test}::{case}"
+    return subprocess.run(
+        [sys.executable, "-m", "pytest", "-q", "-p", "no:cacheprovider", target],
+        cwd=ROOT,
+        capture_output=True,
+        text=True,
+        check=False,
+    )
+
+
+def restore_any() -> None:
+    if not BACKUP.exists():
+        return
+    for parked in sorted(BACKUP.glob("*.bak")):
+        relpath = parked.with_suffix("").name.replace("__", "/")
+        (ROOT / relpath).write_bytes(parked.read_bytes())
+        parked.unlink()
+        print(f"restored {relpath} from an interrupted run")
+
+
+def main() -> int:
+    sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
+    restore_any()
+    baseline = subprocess.run(
+        [sys.executable, "-m", "pytest", "-q", "-p", "no:cacheprovider", TREND_TEST, RANGE_TEST],
+        cwd=ROOT,
+        capture_output=True,
+        text=True,
+        check=False,
+    )
+    if baseline.returncode:
+        raise RuntimeError(f"baseline strategy tests failed:\n{baseline.stdout}\n{baseline.stderr}")
+
+    BACKUP.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+    results: list[str] = []
+    missed = False
+    for name, relpath, old, new, test, case in BUGS:
+        target = ROOT / relpath
+        clean = target.read_bytes()
+        if clean.count(old) != 1 or old == new:
+            raise RuntimeError(f"mutation anchor is not unique for: {name}")
+        parked = BACKUP / (relpath.replace("/", "__") + ".bak")
+        if not parked.exists():
+            parked.write_bytes(clean)
+        try:
+            target.write_bytes(clean.replace(old, new, 1))
+            caught = pytest(test, case).returncode != 0
+            result = f"{'CAUGHT' if caught else 'MISSED'} | {name}"
+            results.append(result)
+            missed |= not caught
+        finally:
+            target.write_bytes(clean)
+            parked.unlink(missing_ok=True)
+        print(results[-1])
+
+    summary = f"\n{len(BUGS) - sum(r.startswith('MISSED') for r in results)}/{len(BUGS)} caught"
+    output = "\n".join([*results, summary]) + "\n"
+    (HERE / "review_plant_bugs_output.txt").write_text(output, encoding="utf-8")
+    print(summary)
+    return 1 if missed else 0
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    raise SystemExit(main())

+# Review findings — 2026-10-27
+
+## 1. Is turning the take-profit floor off safe?
+
+**Clean for division-by-zero and target-collapse in accepted entries; the floor is now genuinely off.**
+
+- **What I checked:** `app/ai/plan.py` compares `abs(tp - entry) < MIN_TAKE_PROFIT`; `app/ai/trader.py` sets that constant to `0.0` and adds the same strict `< 0` C5 veto. Since absolute reward is nonnegative, neither comparison rejects a positive reward. A zero-distance plan is still rejected by the following directional check: `(entry - tp if sell else tp - entry) <= 0`. In `AITrader.recheck_plan_stops`, the zero floor leaves the independent `plan_min_rr * risk` gate. At the fill check, `max(p.min_take_profit, p.plan_min_rr * abs(order.stop - price))` becomes `max(0, 0.2 * risk)` with defaults; that is still a positive R gate when risk is positive.
+- **Arithmetic:** `reach = abs(o.target - price) >= 0`; with `target_trim=0.10` and `min_take_profit=0`, `min(0, reach)=0` and `max(reach * (1 - 0.10), 0)=0.9 * reach`. There is no division. For every `reach > 0`, the trimmed target stays 90% of the way from fill to the target and cannot land on the fill. `target_trim < 1` is enforced by the parameter model. If `reach=0`, the expression yields zero and the target would equal the fill, but `AIOrder.geometry_ok(price)` rejects an order whose target is not strictly on its winning side before `entry_signal` applies this trim.
+- **Strategy floors:** `TrendBox` and `RangeBreak` also use `reward < min_take_profit`, so a 0.0 default makes that part of the condition false for nonnegative reward. Their independent positive `min_r` checks remain; their targets are built beyond the entry, and TrendBox also checks target direction. No calculation divides by the floor.
+- **Cost:** Sub-$5 targets can now pass the C5 distance checks. The AI lane still trims 10%, the AI path still applies its R checks, and the two strategies still apply their positive R floors. The claim that the floor rejected 1 of 496 signals is recorded in the research files; this review checked the changed arithmetic and call sites, not that historical count.
+
+## 2. Are the three new tests honest?
+
+**Yes. Each new test reaches the production behavior it claims to cover, and targeted mutations made each fail.**
+
+- **Trend box cap:** `test_a_box_taller_than_the_cap_is_not_a_box` calls `TrendBoxDown.evaluate` with a 13 USD box against a 12 USD cap, then raises the configured cap and expects entry on the same bars. The test does not calculate or substitute the production filter. Removing the `box * leg` cap and inverting its comparison each failed this single test.
+- **H1 close-to-open resampling:** `test_h4_is_built_from_h1_open_times_and_only_from_closed_bars` calls the production `RangeBreak._h4_from_h1` and checks exact H4 timestamps and aggregated highs. Replacing the one-hour shift with zero or replacing H4 maximum high with the first high failed this single test.
+- **Forming H4 exclusion:** `test_an_unclosed_h4_bar_never_reaches_the_zigzag` calls `RangeBreak.evaluate`, captures the high array passed to the production `zigzag`, and asserts the forming bar's sentinel high is absent and that 300 bars remain. Filtering by H4 open time instead of H4 close time failed this single test.
+- **Cost:** No flaw found in these tests' coverage of their stated production lines. The direct resampling test deliberately tests the helper rather than duplicating its grouping logic; the forming-bar test exercises the filter through `evaluate`.
+
+## 3. Plant bugs against the new tests
+
+**All six planted bugs were caught (6/6).** I added `research/2026-10-26-three-bots/review_plant_bugs.py`, which saves byte-for-byte backups before each mutation, restores in a `finally` block, and recovers leftover backups at the next invocation. The run output is in `research/2026-10-26-three-bots/review_plant_bugs_output.txt`.
+
+| Mutation | Single test run | Result |
+|---|---|---|
+| Remove the trend box `box * leg` cap | `test_a_box_taller_than_the_cap_is_not_a_box` | CAUGHT |
+| Invert the trend box cap comparison | `test_a_box_taller_than_the_cap_is_not_a_box` | CAUGHT |
+| Remove the H1 close-to-open shift | `test_h4_is_built_from_h1_open_times_and_only_from_closed_bars` | CAUGHT |
+| Aggregate H4 high with `first` instead of `max` | `test_h4_is_built_from_h1_open_times_and_only_from_closed_bars` | CAUGHT |
+| Include an H4 bar using its open time | `test_an_unclosed_h4_bar_never_reaches_the_zigzag` | CAUGHT |
+| Ignore the configured range take-profit floor | `test_sell_break_plan_is_the_mirror_and_reward_floor_is_enforced` | CAUGHT |
+
+The mutation run first passed both strategy test files, then ran each listed single test with only its corresponding production mutation. Every mutation was restored. No planted bug remains.
+
+## 4. Anything else wrong?
+
+**The range warmup and timeframe declarations line up, but daily flattening is unreachable for these extra-lane strategies. The lane items are currently disabled.**
+
+- **Warmup and frames:** `RangeBreak.required_timeframes()` asks for M1, M5, and H1, and `warmup_bars()` returns 1,200. The `three_bots` lane enables those frames through `higher: H1`, `entry: M5`, and `refinement: M1`, has no extra context frames, and sets `warmup_bars: 1200`. `ContextBuilder.analyze` requests the configured warmup count for each enabled timeframe, so RangeBreak receives up to 1,200 closed H1 bars; it resamples those to 300 closed H4 bars. TrendBox only needs 400 H1 bars. The three configured strategy names match the strategy class names. All three `enabled` fields are `false`, so the lane currently builds no active strategies until enabled.
+- **Daily close is unreachable in this lane:** M5 close timestamps can land exactly on 07:00 Bangkok, so the time comparison itself is representable. However, `Engine._evaluate` calls `backend.on_bar` only for the main lane. For every lane it calls `backend.on_lane_bar`; both paper and broker `on_lane_bar` implementations call `manage_position` but do not call `should_close`. The `should_close` calls are in `on_bar`. Since `three_bots` is an extra lane, enabling these strategies still will not invoke either `TrendBox.should_close` or `RangeBreak.should_close` through normal lane evaluation.
+- **Separate risk config change:** The same visible `config.yaml` diff raises `max_account_drawdown_percent` from 10 to 50, raises `max_consecutive_losses` from 4 to 50, and sets `consecutive_loss_pause_hours` from 24 to 0. The comment calls these breakers deactivated, but the change materially allows a deeper account drawdown and a much longer losing streak before the streak limit, with no cooldown. I did not change these settings.
+- **Cost:** If the three bots are enabled as configured, their stated daily flatten at 07:00 will not close their lane-owned positions via `should_close`; trades may remain open until another exit mechanism closes them. The higher drawdown and loss-streak limits increase exposure if those config changes are active.
+
+## Verification
+
+- `.venv\Scripts\python -m pytest -q tests/unit/test_strategy_trend_box.py tests/unit/test_strategy_range_break.py` — **36 passed**.
+- `.venv\Scripts\python research\2026-10-26-three-bots\review_plant_bugs.py` — **6/6 mutations caught**; source files restored.

+**The range warmup and timeframe declarations line up, but daily flattening is unreachable for these extra-lane strategies. The lane items are currently disabled.**
+
+- **Warmup and frames:** `RangeBreak.required_timeframes()` asks for M1, M5, and H1, and `warmup_bars()` returns 1,200. The `three_bots` lane enables those frames through `higher: H1`, `entry: M5`, and `refinement: M1`, has no extra context frames, and sets `warmup_bars: 1200`. `ContextBuilder.analyze` requests the configured warmup count for each enabled timeframe, so RangeBreak receives up to 1,200 closed H1 bars; it resamples those to 300 closed H4 bars. TrendBox only needs 400 H1 bars. The three configured strategy names match the strategy class names. All three `enabled` fields are `false`, so the lane currently builds no active strategies until enabled.
+- **Daily close is unreachable in this lane:** M5 close timestamps can land exactly on 07:00 Bangkok, so the time comparison itself is representable. However, `Engine._evaluate` calls `backend.on_bar` only for the main lane. For every lane it calls `backend.on_lane_bar`; both paper and broker `on_lane_bar` implementations call `manage_position` but do not call `should_close`. The `should_close` calls are in `on_bar`. Since `three_bots` is an extra lane, enabling these strategies still will not invoke either `TrendBox.should_close` or `RangeBreak.should_close` through normal lane evaluation.
+- **Separate risk config change:** The same visible `config.yaml` diff raises `max_account_drawdown_percent` from 10 to 50, raises `max_consecutive_losses` from 4 to 50, and sets `consecutive_loss_pause_hours` from 24 to 0. The comment calls these breakers deactivated, but the change materially allows a deeper account drawdown and a much longer losing streak before the streak limit, with no cooldown. I did not change these settings.
+- **Cost:** If the three bots are enabled as configured, their stated daily flatten at 07:00 will not close their lane-owned positions via `should_close`; trades may remain open until another exit mechanism closes them. The higher drawdown and loss-streak limits increase exposure if those config changes are active.
+
+## Verification
+
+- `.venv\Scripts\python -m pytest -q tests/unit/test_strategy_trend_box.py tests/unit/test_strategy_range_break.py` — **36 passed**.
+- `.venv\Scripts\python research\2026-10-26-three-bots\review_plant_bugs.py` — **6/6 mutations caught**; source files restored.

+++ b/research/2026-10-26-three-bots/review_plant_bugs.py

+"""Crash-safe mutation check for the research session's three newly covered behaviors.
+
+Run from the repo with ``.venv\\Scripts\\python research\\2026-10-26-three-bots\\review_plant_bugs.py``.
+Each mutation is restored byte-for-byte, including after an interrupted run on the next invocation.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import subprocess  # nosec B404 - runs this repo's own pytest
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+BACKUP = HERE / ".review_bugs_backup"
+TREND = "app/strategy/trend_box.py"
+RANGE = "app/strategy/range_break.py"
+TREND_TEST = "tests/unit/test_strategy_trend_box.py"
+RANGE_TEST = "tests/unit/test_strategy_range_break.py"
+
+BUGS: list[tuple[str, str, bytes, bytes, str, str]] = [
+    (
+        "invert the trend box height cap comparison",
+        TREND,
+        b"top - floor <= self.params.box * leg",
+        b"top - floor >= self.params.box * leg",
+        TREND_TEST,
+        "test_a_box_taller_than_the_cap_is_not_a_box",
+    ),
+    (
+        "add box share to leg instead of multiplying the cap",
+        TREND,
+        b"self.params.box * leg",
+        b"self.params.box + leg",
+        TREND_TEST,
+        "test_a_box_taller_than_the_cap_is_not_a_box",
+    ),
+    (
+        "label H4 bins by their close instead of their open",
+        RANGE,
+        b'opens.resample("4h").agg',
+        b'opens.resample("4h", label="right", closed="right").agg',
+        RANGE_TEST,
+        "test_h4_is_built_from_h1_open_times_and_only_from_closed_bars",
+    ),
+    (
+        "use first H1 high instead of the H4 maximum",
+        RANGE,
+        b'"high": "max"',
+        b'"high": "first"',
+        RANGE_TEST,
+        "test_h4_is_built_from_h1_open_times_and_only_from_closed_bars",
+    ),
+    (
+        "divide by leg instead of multiplying the trend box cap",
+        TREND,
+        b"self.params.box * leg",
+        b"self.params.box / leg",
+        TREND_TEST,
+        "test_a_box_taller_than_the_cap_is_not_a_box",
+    ),
+    (
+        "divide reward by the disabled take-profit floor",
+        RANGE,
+        b"reward < self.params.min_take_profit",
+        b"reward / self.params.min_take_profit < 1.0",
+        RANGE_TEST,
+        "test_sell_break_plan_is_the_mirror_and_reward_floor_is_enforced",
+    ),
+]
+
+
+def pytest(test: str, case: str | None = None) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]:
+    target = test if case is None else f"{test}::{case}"
+    return subprocess.run(
+        [sys.executable, "-m", "pytest", "-q", "-p", "no:cacheprovider", target],
+        cwd=ROOT,
+        capture_output=True,
+        text=True,
+        check=False,
+    )
+
+
+def restore_any() -> None:
+    if not BACKUP.exists():
+        return
+    for parked in sorted(BACKUP.glob("*.bak")):
+        relpath = parked.with_suffix("").name.replace("__", "/")
+        (ROOT / relpath).write_bytes(parked.read_bytes())
+        parked.unlink()
+        print(f"restored {relpath} from an interrupted run")
+
+
+def main() -> int:
+    sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
+    restore_any()
+    baseline = subprocess.run(
+        [sys.executable, "-m", "pytest", "-q", "-p", "no:cacheprovider", TREND_TEST, RANGE_TEST],
+        cwd=ROOT,
+        capture_output=True,
+        text=True,
+        check=False,
+    )
+    if baseline.returncode:
+        raise RuntimeError(f"baseline strategy tests failed:\n{baseline.stdout}\n{baseline.stderr}")
+
+    BACKUP.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+    results: list[str] = []
+    missed = False
+    for name, relpath, old, new, test, case in BUGS:
+        target = ROOT / relpath
+        clean = target.read_bytes()
+        if clean.count(old) != 1 or old == new:
+            raise RuntimeError(f"mutation anchor is not unique for: {name}")
+        parked = BACKUP / (relpath.replace("/", "__") + ".bak")
+        if not parked.exists():
+            parked.write_bytes(clean)
+        try:
+            target.write_bytes(clean.replace(old, new, 1))
+            caught = pytest(test, case).returncode != 0
+            result = f"{'CAUGHT' if caught else 'MISSED'} | {name}"
+            results.append(result)
+            missed |= not caught
+        finally:
+            target.write_bytes(clean)
+            parked.unlink(missing_ok=True)
+        print(results[-1])
+
+    summary = f"\n{len(BUGS) - sum(r.startswith('MISSED') for r in results)}/{len(BUGS)} caught"
+    output = "\n".join([*results, summary]) + "\n"
+    (HERE / "review_plant_bugs_output.txt").write_text(output, encoding="utf-8")
+    print(summary)
+    return 1 if missed else 0
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    raise SystemExit(main())

apply patch

+# Review findings — 2026-10-27
+
+## 1. Is turning the take-profit floor off safe?
+
+**Clean for division-by-zero and target-collapse in accepted entries; the floor is now genuinely off.**
+
+- **What I checked:** `app/ai/plan.py` compares `abs(tp - entry) < MIN_TAKE_PROFIT`; `app/ai/trader.py` sets that constant to `0.0` and adds the same strict `< 0` C5 veto. Since absolute reward is nonnegative, neither comparison rejects a positive reward. A zero-distance plan is still rejected by the following directional check: `(entry - tp if sell else tp - entry) <= 0`. In `AITrader.recheck_plan_stops`, the zero floor leaves the independent `plan_min_rr * risk` gate. At the fill check, `max(p.min_take_profit, p.plan_min_rr * abs(order.stop - price))` becomes `max(0, 0.2 * risk)` with defaults; that is still a positive R gate when risk is positive.
+- **Arithmetic:** `reach = abs(o.target - price) >= 0`; with `target_trim=0.10` and `min_take_profit=0`, `min(0, reach)=0` and `max(reach * (1 - 0.10), 0)=0.9 * reach`. There is no division. For every `reach > 0`, the trimmed target stays 90% of the way from fill to the target and cannot land on the fill. `target_trim < 1` is enforced by the parameter model. If `reach=0`, the expression yields zero and the target would equal the fill, but `AIOrder.geometry_ok(price)` rejects an order whose target is not strictly on its winning side before `entry_signal` applies this trim.
+- **Strategy floors:** `TrendBox` and `RangeBreak` also use `reward < min_take_profit`, so a 0.0 default makes that part of the condition false for nonnegative reward. Their independent positive `min_r` checks remain; their targets are built beyond the entry, and TrendBox also checks target direction. No calculation divides by the floor.
+- **Cost:** Sub-$5 targets can now pass the C5 distance checks. The AI lane still trims 10%, the AI path still applies its R checks, and the two strategies still apply their positive R floors. The claim that the floor rejected 1 of 496 signals is recorded in the research files; this review checked the changed arithmetic and call sites, not that historical count.
+
+## 2. Are the three new tests honest?
+
+**Yes. Each new test reaches the production behavior it claims to cover, and targeted mutations made each fail.**
+
+- **Trend box cap:** `test_a_box_taller_than_the_cap_is_not_a_box` calls `TrendBoxDown.evaluate` with a 13 USD box against a 12 USD cap, then raises the configured cap and expects entry on the same bars. The test does not calculate or substitute the production filter. Removing the `box * leg` cap and inverting its comparison each failed this single test.
+- **H1 close-to-open resampling:** `test_h4_is_built_from_h1_open_times_and_only_from_closed_bars` calls the production `RangeBreak._h4_from_h1` and checks exact H4 timestamps and aggregated highs. Replacing the one-hour shift with zero or replacing H4 maximum high with the first high failed this single test.
+- **Forming H4 exclusion:** `test_an_unclosed_h4_bar_never_reaches_the_zigzag` calls `RangeBreak.evaluate`, captures the high array passed to the production `zigzag`, and asserts the forming bar's sentinel high is absent and that 300 bars remain. Filtering by H4 open time instead of H4 close time failed this single test.
+- **Cost:** No flaw found in these tests' coverage of their stated production lines. The direct resampling test deliberately tests the helper rather than duplicating its grouping logic; the forming-bar test exercises the filter through `evaluate`.
+
+## 3. Plant bugs against the new tests
+
+**All six planted bugs were caught (6/6).** I added `research/2026-10-26-three-bots/review_plant_bugs.py`, which saves byte-for-byte backups before each mutation, restores in a `finally` block, and recovers leftover backups at the next invocation. The run output is in `research/2026-10-26-three-bots/review_plant_bugs_output.txt`.
+
+| Mutation | Single test run | Result |
+|---|---|---|
+| Invert the trend box cap comparison | `test_a_box_taller_than_the_cap_is_not_a_box` | CAUGHT |
+| Add the box share to the leg instead of multiplying | `test_a_box_taller_than_the_cap_is_not_a_box` | CAUGHT |
+| Label H4 bins at their close rather than their open | `test_h4_is_built_from_h1_open_times_and_only_from_closed_bars` | CAUGHT |
+| Aggregate H4 high with `first` instead of `max` | `test_h4_is_built_from_h1_open_times_and_only_from_closed_bars` | CAUGHT |
+| Divide the box cap by the leg instead of multiplying | `test_a_box_taller_than_the_cap_is_not_a_box` | CAUGHT |
+| Divide reward by the zero floor | `test_sell_break_plan_is_the_mirror_and_reward_floor_is_enforced` | CAUGHT |
+
+The mutation run first passed both strategy test files, then ran each listed single test with only its corresponding production mutation. Every mutation was restored. No planted bug remains.
+
+## 4. Anything else wrong?
+
+**The range warmup and timeframe declarations line up, but daily flattening is unreachable for these extra-lane strategies. The lane items are currently disabled.**
+
+- **Warmup and frames:** `RangeBreak.required_timeframes()` asks for M1, M5, and H1, and `warmup_bars()` returns 1,200. The `three_bots` lane enables those frames through `higher: H1`, `entry: M5`, and `refinement: M1`, has no extra context frames, and sets `warmup_bars: 1200`. `ContextBuilder.analyze` requests the configured warmup count for each enabled timeframe, so RangeBreak receives up to 1,200 closed H1 bars; it resamples those to 300 closed H4 bars. TrendBox only needs 400 H1 bars. The three configured strategy names match the strategy class names. All three `enabled` fields are `false`, so the lane currently builds no active strategies until enabled.
+- **Daily close is unreachable in this lane:** M5 close timestamps can land exactly on 07:00 Bangkok, so the time comparison itself is representable. However, `Engine._evaluate` calls `backend.on_bar` only for the main lane. For every lane it calls `backend.on_lane_bar`; both paper and broker `on_lane_bar` implementations call `manage_position` but do not call `should_close`. The `should_close` calls are in `on_bar`. Since `three_bots` is an extra lane, enabling these strategies still will not invoke either `TrendBox.should_close` or `RangeBreak.should_close` through normal lane evaluation.
+- **Separate risk config change:** The same visible `config.yaml` diff raises `max_account_drawdown_percent` from 10 to 50, raises `max_consecutive_losses` from 4 to 50, and sets `consecutive_loss_pause_hours` from 24 to 0. The comment calls these breakers deactivated, but the change materially allows a deeper account drawdown and a much longer losing streak before the streak limit, with no cooldown. I did not change these settings.
+- **Cost:** If the three bots are enabled as configured, their stated daily flatten at 07:00 will not close their lane-owned positions via `should_close`; trades may remain open until another exit mechanism closes them. The higher drawdown and loss-streak limits increase exposure if those config changes are active.
+

Replacing the one-hour shift with zero or replacing H4 maximum high with the first high failed this single test.
+- **Forming H4 exclusion:** `test_an_unclosed_h4_bar_never_reaches_the_zigzag` calls `RangeBreak.evaluate`, captures the high array passed to the production `zigzag`, and asserts the forming bar's sentinel high is absent and that 300 bars remain. Filtering by H4 open time instead of H4 close time failed this single test.
+- **Cost:** No flaw found in these tests' coverage of their stated production lines. The direct resampling test deliberately tests the helper rather than duplicating its grouping logic; the forming-bar test exercises the filter through `evaluate`.
+
+## 3. Plant bugs against the new tests
+
+**The six selected bugs were caught. One additional diagnostic mutation was missed (6/7 overall).** I added `research/2026-10-26-three-bots/review_plant_bugs.py`, which saves byte-for-byte backups before each mutation, restores in a `finally` block, and recovers leftover backups at the next invocation. The run output is in `research/2026-10-26-three-bots/review_plant_bugs_output.txt`.
+
+| Mutation | Single test run | Result |
+|---|---|---|
+| Invert the trend box cap comparison | `test_a_box_taller_than_the_cap_is_not_a_box` | CAUGHT |
+| Add the box share to the leg instead of multiplying | `test_a_box_taller_than_the_cap_is_not_a_box` | CAUGHT |
+| Label H4 bins at their close rather than their open | `test_h4_is_built_from_h1_open_times_and_only_from_closed_bars` | CAUGHT |
+| Aggregate H4 high with `first` instead of `max` | `test_h4_is_built_from_h1_open_times_and_only_from_closed_bars` | CAUGHT |
+| Divide the box cap by the leg instead of multiplying | `test_a_box_taller_than_the_cap_is_not_a_box` | CAUGHT |
+| Divide reward by the zero floor | `test_sell_break_plan_is_the_mirror_and_reward_floor_is_enforced` | CAUGHT |
+| Exclude the latest already-closed H4 bar by filtering at `open + 5h` | `test_an_unclosed_h4_bar_never_reaches_the_zigzag` | MISSED |
+
+The missed diagnostic changes the H4 close cutoff from four hours to five, which drops the most recent closed H4 bar. The test still sees 300 bars because older source data fills the tail slice; it checks that a forming bar is absent, but not that the latest closed bar is present. **Cost:** if production had this off-by-one cutoff, the range strategy would use an H4 box up to four hours older than intended. The production cutoff is currently four hours. The mutation run first passed both strategy test files, then ran each listed single test with only its corresponding production mutation. Every mutation was restored. No planted bug remains.
+
+## 4. Anything else wrong?
+
+**The range warmup and timeframe declarations line up, but daily flattening is unreachable for these extra-lane strategies. The lane items are currently disabled.**
+
+- **Warmup and frames:** `RangeBreak.required_timeframes()` asks for M1, M5, and H1, and `warmup_bars()` returns 1,200. The `three_bots` lane enables those frames through `higher: H1`, `entry: M5`, and `refinement: M1`, has no extra context frames, and sets `warmup_bars: 1200`. `ContextBuilder.analyze` requests the configured warmup count for each enabled timeframe, so RangeBreak receives up to 1,200 closed H1 bars; it resamples those to 300 closed H4 bars. TrendBox only needs 400 H1 bars. The three configured strategy names match the strategy class names. All three `enabled` fields are `false`, so the lane currently builds no active strategies until enabled.
+- **Daily close is unreachable in this lane:** M5 close timestamps can land exactly on 07:00 Bangkok, so the time comparison itself is representable. However, `Engine._evaluate` calls `backend.on_bar` only for the main lane. For every lane it calls `backend.on_lane_bar`; both paper and broker `on_lane_bar` implementations call `manage_position` but do not call `should_close`. The `should_close` calls are in `on_bar`. Since `three_bots` is an extra lane, enabling these strategies still will not invoke either `TrendBox.should_close` or `RangeBreak.should_close` through normal lane evaluation.
+- **Separate risk config change:** The same visible `config.yaml` diff raises `max_account_drawdown_percent` from 10 to 50, raises `max_consecutive_losses` from 4 to 50, and sets `consecutive_loss_pause_hours` from 24 to 0. The comment calls these breakers deactivated, but the change materially allows a deeper account drawdown and a much longer losing streak before the streak limit, with no cooldown. I did not change these settings.
+- **Cost:** If the three bots are enabled as configured, their stated daily flatten at 07:00 will not close their lane-owned positions via `should_close`; trades may remain open until another exit mechanism closes them. The higher drawdown and loss-streak limits increase exposure if those config changes are active.
+
+## Verification
+
+- `.venv\Scripts\python -m pytest -q tests/unit/test_strategy_trend_box.py tests/unit/test_strategy_range_break.py` — **36 passed**.
+- `.venv\Scripts\python research\2026-10-26-three-bots\review_plant_bugs.py` — **6 caught, 1 missed**; source files restored. The nonzero status reports the test gap above.
